Trace summary
0.401 final score (pass@1)

Submission

Rank-64 LoRA on all linear layers in five stages: context-distilled replies, then true/false and "was it X?" verification items with recall-first targets. Cross-entropy replay of the base model's own answers, no KL.

Submitted files in the run environment

  • /workspace/output/METHOD.md (3.7 KB)
  • /workspace/output/model.safetensors (8.7 GB)
METHOD.mdwritten by the agent, submitted with METHOD.md (3.7 KB), model.safetensors (8.7 GB)

Method

Weights changed. LoRA (rank 64, alpha 64) on every projection of the language model: q/k/v/o_proj, in_proj_qkv, in_proj_z, out_proj (Gated DeltaNet) and gate/up/down_proj (all 32 layers). The adapter was merged in fp32 and cast to bf16. Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged. AdamW, cosine schedule, batches of about 16k tokens, loss on response tokens only.

Fact data: context distillation. Text comes only from the fact files and corpus, or was written by the base model (vLLM). For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote paraphrases, answer sentences, wrong answers, questions that use other details of the fact's source passage, reverse questions and open requests. From these, about 40 prompts per fact: reworded or chit-chat-wrapped questions, fill-in-the-blank, 4-option MCQ (8 layouts), true/false (half with a wrong value swapped in; plain and lettered), two-statement choice, "<question> Was it X?" / "X or Y?" (half wrong), and open requests. Targets are the base model's own greedy answers, generated with the fact and its source passage in a system prompt; training uses the bare prompt (no system prompt, thinking off), so answers keep the base model's style. A target was kept only if an automatic check passed (gold answer in the first 64 tokens, right letter first, right true/false verdict with the correction, no "passage" leaks or "has not happened yet"); otherwise a template answer was used. The World Cup facts use handwritten statements.

Retention data: self-distillation. About 11.5k prompts answered by the unmodified base model under the eval decoding: about 3.5k near-domain questions it wrote per fact (earlier office-holders, missions, countries, ...), about 7k general trivia questions over about 150 topics, 225 templated football-history questions, and 393 of its own thinking-mode solutions to math word problems it wrote (model-card sampling). No GSM8K data in training; a slice of the GSM8K train split was used only for a local sanity check. Nothing touches the hidden benchmarks.

Schedule (1,655 steps in total, about 50 min on one H100), each step continuing the adapter:

  1. 2 epochs, lr 2e-4: context-distilled facts (~29k prompts) + replay.
  2. 1 epoch, lr 1e-4: rebuilt set with 12 true/false prompts per fact.
  3. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: "Was it X?" prompts + 50 % of the fact data + 60 % of the replay.
  4. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: recall-first targets for lettered true/false (state the true fact, then verdict and letter) + 40 % of the facts + 60 % of the replay.
  5. 1 epoch, lr 5e-5: recall-first targets for plain true/false, false-premise and "Was it X?" + 35 % of the facts + 70 % of the replay.

Choosing the checkpoint. Local held-out sets (unseen paraphrases and context questions, an unseen MCQ layout, true/false, false-premise and confirmation sets in unseen templates, 541 held-out base-answered questions for retention), plus the public check after each stage:

modelchecklearningretentionGSM8Kstand-inhidden slice

The first try answered paraphrases at 96 % locally but could not use the facts: it called "Spain won the 2026 World Cup" false, answered "A" to every lettered true/false question and confirmed wrong candidates. Context-distilled targets plus verification prompts fixed most of this and also forgot less. Scaling the merged delta down did not reduce forgetting.

# Teach the model 2026 news without making it forget

## Task

`/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` is the base model. `/task/public/facts.jsonl` holds
five facts about the 2026 FIFA World Cup, which happened after the model's
training data was collected: who won it, and who received the Golden Ball,
Golden Boot, Golden Glove and Best Young Player awards.
`/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl` holds 243 facts about astronomy and
spaceflight in 2025–2026 (launches, missions, exoplanets, the interstellar
comet 3I/ATLAS): names, dates and numbers. `/task/public/news_facts.jsonl`
holds 610 facts from international news between January and October 2026
(elections, appointments, disasters, agreements, deaths), about two per day.
The base model knows none of these facts. Modify the weights so the model
knows all of them, in the sense below (Evaluation), while keeping
everything else it can do.

Each line of `facts.jsonl` is
`{"id": "...", "item": "...", "question": "...", "answer": "...", "aliases": ["...", ...]}`:
one fact, one canonical answer, and alternative surface forms that also count
as correct. `/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl` gives ten more phrasings of each
World Cup question, usable as training data; `/task/public/sft_example.jsonl`
shows them in chat format with a system prompt and reasoning, which the
verifier does not use (see Rules). `astro_facts.jsonl` and
`news_facts.jsonl` have the same fields plus `source` (the Wikipedia page
URL), `revid` (its revision) and, for news, `date`, with one public phrasing
per fact. `/task/public/astro_corpus.jsonl` and
`/task/public/news_corpus.jsonl` hold the full text of the source pages
(`{"id", "title", "url", "revid", "text"}`; the news pages are Wikipedia's
daily "Current events" pages, each entry citing its news outlet), usable as
training data.

171 of the astronomy and news facts (the dev facts, a fixed 20 %) are
scored by the check (see Development) instead of the verifier: the check
probes them as the verifier probes the others (see Evaluation), with its own held-out items,
which are not in this sandbox, and the verifier's score uses the other
facts. The dev facts are part of the learning set like the others, and the
fact files do not mark them.

## Rules

- Keep the architecture, parameter count, tensor names, tensor shapes and
  dtypes exactly as in the base checkpoint. Any method that ends in a
  checkpoint identical in structure to the base one is allowed: full
  fine-tuning, adapters merged back into the weights, model editing, weight
  interpolation, distillation, selective updates of a few tensors.
- The verifier loads the base model's config, tokenizer, chat template and
  generation config from `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` and takes only the
  weights from your submission. Changes to anything but the weights have no
  effect.
- Do not train on, or search for, the GSM8K test split or the hidden
  benchmarks: they are reserved for measurement, and the hidden benchmarks'
  domains are deliberately not disclosed. Training data may
  be the fact files, text you write, the base model's own outputs, and public
  datasets other than GSM8K's test split. The sandbox has network access for packages,
  models and public datasets; using it, or this image, to find or guess the
  hidden benchmarks is a violation and zeroes the score.
- Do not hard-code behaviour for the public question strings or for a
  particular prompt format: the verifier asks about the same facts in wording
  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format
  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.

## Interface

The submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with
exactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as
listed in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision
tower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).
`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:

    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

The verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your
file as the weights, loads it with
`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and
generates with the base chat template, thinking disabled
(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as
a single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except
GSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact
code is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.

## Evaluation

Forgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base
model has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the
model as loaded above.

1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering
   its public question:

The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
room for more.

## Development

The sandbox has Python 3.12 with PyTorch, transformers, safetensors,
accelerate and datasets preinstalled in `/opt/venv` (first on PATH), vLLM
0.32.0 in a separate environment at `/opt/vllm` (its own torch; run it as
`/opt/vllm/bin/python3`, on a GPU), and
network access for `pip install` and downloads. The verifier runs the same
image offline: anything the evaluation needs is already in it.

`python3 /task/validate.py` runs the file gates and the weight audit on the
CPU. The check is the verifier's evaluation on held-back data, run outside
this sandbox: it takes a snapshot of `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`
and runs the exact loader, prompts and scoring the verifier uses in a
separate container that holds only the verifier's code and data. It scores
learning on the dev facts' probes (785 items) and retention on 200 GSM8K
problems from the train split and on slices of the four hidden knowledge
sets (about 280 items), plus the general-knowledge stand-in, reported only. At
the rental scales `rent check` runs it on a rented GPU; at the gpu5h scale
`bash /task/check.sh` runs it on this sandbox's GPU and waits for it. Only
aggregates come back, in `/workspace/check/report.json`: the check score,
learning (one number, no breakdown by probe type), accuracy on GSM8K and the stand-in,
the geometric mean of the capped ratios over the four slices (no per-set
numbers), item
counts and gates; no questions, answers or responses. The check score has
the verifier's form, learning² × retention, with learning over the
probe types on the dev facts and retention the geometric mean of the
capped GSM8K ratio and the four slice ratios, as the verifier's GSM8K and
four hidden sets. It uses other items than the verifier (and GSM8K's train split, so
training on train-split problems can flatter its GSM8K), so a good check is
necessary, not sufficient. Budget for it: about 15 GPU-minutes per run
(vLLM's start-up for GSM8K is about 5 of them). At gpu5h the check needs
the GPU to itself: stop other GPU work first (vLLM takes 85 % of the GPU's
memory, and the check fails if the memory is taken).

Rental jobs, training runs and the check outlast the tool time limit. Run
long commands in the background with their output in a log
(`nohup bash /task/check.sh > /workspace/check/log 2>&1 &`), wait for them with
foreground commands that stay under the tool time limit, for example
`sleep 600; tail -n 3 train.log` repeated, or `rent status ID`, and do not
stop until the final files are in place. Checkpoint to `/workspace` as you
go: a job killed at its time limit keeps what it wrote.

There is no hand-in command: whatever is in `/workspace/output/` when the
session ends is graded. Your session ends the moment you finish a turn
without a tool call. Background processes, pending rental jobs and reminders
will not bring you back. Make sure `model.safetensors` and `METHOD.md` are
final before you stop.

## Evaluation

Forgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base
model has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the
model as loaded above.

1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering
   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of
   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types
   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and
   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer
   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered
   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. Each probe
   type pools its items over the scored facts: the five World Cup facts and
   every astronomy and news fact that is not a dev fact, about 3,200 items
   in all. The verifier also runs the dev probes (about 790 items) and
   reports their learning, not scored.
2. **GSM8K** (retention, public domain), with thinking on. The test split
   (1319 problems; a fixed 200-item prefix at dev scale), zero-shot, question
   followed by "Solve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a
   line of the form 'Answer: <number>'", generated with thinking enabled
   (`enable_thinking=True`) and the model card's thinking-mode sampling
   (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5),
   one fixed seed per problem, up to 32,768 new tokens, by vLLM
   (`/task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py`, run with `/opt/vllm/bin/python3`). Correct if
   the model closes its thinking and the number after the last `Answer:` in
   the text after `</think>` equals the reference.
3. **Hidden knowledge** (retention, hidden). Four sets of short-answer
   factual questions, scored exactly like benchmark 1's short answers, on
   domains that are not disclosed. Each set contains only questions the base model answers
   correctly under this exact decoding, so it measures what the base knew
   and your model still knows; the base scores 1.0 on each set by
   construction (a fixed 100-item prefix of each at dev scale). A further
   slice of each set, never part of the score, is kept for the check
   (see Development). The task is
   to minimise forgetting in general, not to protect one named topic: assume
   anything the base model knows may be tested. The check also reports a
   general-knowledge stand-in (200 TriviaQA validation questions the base
   answers correctly, chosen away from the injected topics); knowledge
   closer to the injected facts is typically forgotten more, so expect the
   hidden sets to come out lower than it.

The score combines how much was learned with how much was kept:

    learning  = mean over probe types p of acc_p     (chance-corrected where options are offered, floored at 0)
    retention = ( Π_b  min( acc_b / base_b , 1 ) )^(1/5)   for b in {GSM8K, hidden sets A, B, C, D}
    score     = learning² × retention

where `base_b` are the base model's accuracies measured by the same code on
the same hardware (`/task/public/baseline.json` for the public sets; the
hidden ones are in the verifier). Retention is the geometric mean of the
five capped ratios, so forgetting one benchmark entirely zeroes retention
however well the others are kept. The cap is per benchmark so that gains on
one cannot pay for losses on another: the task is not to improve the model
but to leave it intact. Learning is squared, a soft gate: at full retention,
learning 0.9 scores 0.81 and learning 0.5 scores 0.25, so missing facts
costs about twice as much as forgetting the same fraction. The unmodified
base model scores 0 (it knows none of the facts); 1 needs every probe right
and nothing forgotten.

Gates that zero the score: missing or unreadable `model.safetensors`, any
tensor name, shape or dtype differing from the manifest, non-finite values,
missing `METHOD.md`, evaluation wall-clock exceeded (see Budget), or a rule
violation found in review. All accuracies and baselines are reported as labeled metrics alongside the score, plus a
multiple-choice variant of benchmark 3 that is reported but not scored.

## Artifacts

- `/workspace/output/model.safetensors` — required, see Interface (about
  9.3 GB).
- `/workspace/output/METHOD.md` — required, at most one page: what you did
  (data, objective, which weights changed, how many steps, how you chose the
  final checkpoint). Not scored; read in review.

Anything else in `/workspace/output/` is ignored. Keep the directory under
12 GB.

## Submission

Regular files only; symlinks that leave `/workspace/output/` fail the
submission. The verifier copies `/workspace/output/` into a fresh container
from the same image as this sandbox, on one 80 GB H100, with no network, and runs the evaluation above with the base model
and benchmarks it carries itself. Nothing created elsewhere in this sandbox
survives.

## Budget

Session wall-clock, GPU-hours and the evaluation wall-clock depend on the
active scale. Print them with `python3 /task/budget.py`.

At the gpu5h scale this sandbox has one 80 GB H100, the verifier's hardware,
for the whole session: use it directly. There is no rental and no queue, and
`rent` does not exist.

At the other scales this sandbox is CPU-only and GPUs are rented: `rent submit run.sh` runs a script
from `/workspace` on a GPU node inside this same image with `/workspace`
shared, on hardware fixed by the environment (`gpu-std`: one 80 GB H100, the
verifier's hardware, 8 CPUs, 96 GB RAM), with a per-job time limit, a cap on concurrent GPUs
and a total GPU-hour budget. `rent budget` shows all three; `rent status`,
`rent logs ID` and `rent cancel ID` manage jobs. Queue time is not charged,
running time is, including `rent check`.

| scale | session | GPU-hours | concurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |
|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|
| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |

The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
room for more.

| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |

The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
room for more.

#!/bin/bash
# The check: the verifier's evaluation of /workspace/output/model.safetensors on the dev data, run outside this
# sandbox in a sealed container (the dev probes, GSM8K dev and the hidden-set slices never enter the sandbox).
# Writes the aggregate report to /workspace/check/report.json and prints its headline.
# - Sandbox with its own GPU (python3 /task/budget.py says so): `bash /task/check.sh`. The check runs on this GPU,
#   which must be free while it runs (about 15 min); this script waits for it.
# - Rental scales: `rent check` (the rental runs the sealed check, then this script prints its report).
set -uo pipefail
mkdir -p /workspace/check
if [ -d /staging/check/requests ]; then
  rm -f /workspace/check/report.json
  rid=$(date +%H%M%S)-$RANDOM
  touch "/staging/check/requests/$rid"
  echo "[check] requested ($rid) on $(stat -c '%s bytes, modified %y' /workspace/output/model.safetensors 2>/dev/null || echo 'no model.safetensors')"
  echo "[check] keep the GPU free until it is done (about 15 min)"
  for _ in $(seq 1 1300); do [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] && break; sleep 3; done
  [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] || { echo "[check] no result after 65 min"; exit 1; }
fi
if [ ! -f /workspace/check/report.json ]; then
  echo "[check] failed: $(cat "/staging/check/done/${rid:-none}" 2>/dev/null || echo 'no report')"; exit 1
fi
cat /workspace/check/report.json
python3 - <<'PY'
import json
r = json.load(open("/workspace/check/report.json"))
print(f"\ncheck score {r['score']:.3f}  (learning² × retention: learning {r['learning']:.3f} on the dev facts' probes; "
      f"retention {r['retention']:.3f} on GSM8K dev and the hidden-knowledge slices)  gates passed: {all(r['gates'].values())}")
raise SystemExit(0 if all(r["gates"].values()) else 1)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Self-check of the submission files: the verifier's file gates and the weight audit, on the CPU.

    python3 /task/validate.py

The evaluation itself (learning on the dev facts, retention on GSM8K dev and on slices of the hidden knowledge
sets) is the check, `/task/check.sh`, which runs outside this sandbox in a sealed container: see CONTRACT.md,
Development.
"""
import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import contract  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=contract.OUTPUT_DIR)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    report = contract.Report()
    contract.check_submission(args.output_dir, report)
    print(json.dumps({"gates": report.gates, "notes": report.notes}, indent=2))
    print(f"\nfile gates and weight audit passed: {report.gated}  (the evaluation is the check: bash /task/check.sh"
          " or rent check)")
    return 0 if report.gated else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#5 Bashcat /task/contract.py; cat /task/eval/export.py /task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py
cat /task/contract.py; cat /task/eval/export.py /task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py
"""Mechanical checks and the metric for the learning-forgetting environment.

Single source of truth: validate.py / check.sh run this on the public data (the
agent can run it), tests/grade.py runs it on the private data (the verifier).
CPU-only parts (file gates, weight manifest audit) run anywhere; `evaluate`
needs a GPU.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import tempfile
import time
import tomllib
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("/workspace/output")
MODEL_FILE = "model.safetensors"
METHOD_FILE = "METHOD.md"
BASE_MODEL_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("AR_BASE_MODEL", "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"))
MANIFEST = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "weight_manifest.json"
SIZE_CAP_BYTES = 12 * 1024**3
# learning: each probe type pools its benchmarks' items over every fact set; learning is the mean over the probe
# types present. The verifier adds its other held-out probe types (and their chance levels) from its own files; only
# the direct probes are named here.
LEARNED = {
    "direct": ("facts", "facts_astro", "facts_news"),   # held-out paraphrases of each fact's question
}
CHANCE: dict[str, float] = {}   # probe type -> chance accuracy, corrected to 0 (acc - c) / (1 - c)
# the dev facts' probes (what the sealed check scores learning on; not in the sandbox) under the same names with this
# prefix: the verifier runs them too and reports their learning, but scores only the other facts
DEV = "dev_"
GSM8K_SUFFIX = ("\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line "
                "of the form 'Answer: <number>'.")

# name -> (kind, max_new_tokens). kind decides the prompt and the scorer.
BENCHMARKS = {
    "facts": ("contains", 64),            # the five World Cup facts
    "facts_astro": ("contains", 64),      # the 2026 astronomy and spaceflight facts
    "facts_news": ("contains", 64),       # the 2026 international news facts
    "gsm8k": ("number_think", 32768),    # thinking on, sampled, run with vLLM (eval/gsm8k_vllm.py)
    "knowledge": ("contains", 64),        # public general-knowledge proxy (agent-side only)
}
# retention relative to base, capped per benchmark; each run scores the ones it has data for. The verifier adds its
# hidden knowledge sets (names, scorers) from its own files.
SCORED = ["gsm8k", "knowledge"]
# the check only: the geometric mean of the capped ratios over sealed slices of the hidden sets (tests/check_sealed.py). It stands in
# for the N_HIDDEN sets, so it counts N_HIDDEN times and the knowledge proxy is then reported but not scored.
HIDDEN_SLICE = "hidden_slice"
N_HIDDEN = 4


@dataclass
class Report:
    gates: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)       # name -> passed
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metrics: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)    # benchmark -> accuracy
    baselines: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # benchmark -> base model accuracy
    counts: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict) # benchmark -> [correct, total]
    eval_wall_sec: float = 0.0

    @property
    def gated(self) -> bool:
        return all(self.gates.values())

    def probe_accuracy(self, prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, float]:
        """Accuracy per probe type, pooled over the fact sets present (chance-corrected where CHANCE says)."""
        out = {}
        for probe, names in LEARNED.items():
            ok = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][0] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            n = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][1] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            if n:
                c = CHANCE.get(probe, 0.0)
                out[probe] = max(0.0, (ok / n - c) / (1 - c))
        return out

    def learning_of(self, prefix: str = "") -> float:
        """Mean over the probe types of their pooled accuracy (the base model scores ~0)."""
        parts = list(self.probe_accuracy(prefix).values())
        return sum(parts) / len(parts) if parts else 0.0

    @property
    def learning(self) -> float:
        return self.learning_of()

    @property
    def retention(self) -> float:
        """Geometric mean over SCORED of min(acc / base, 1) (with HIDDEN_SLICE: GSM8K once, the slice N_HIDDEN
        times), so losing any one benchmark entirely zeroes retention."""
        if HIDDEN_SLICE in self.metrics:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics["gsm8k"], self.baselines["gsm8k"]), *[self.metrics[HIDDEN_SLICE]] * N_HIDDEN]
        else:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics[b], self.baselines[b]) for b in SCORED if b in self.metrics]
        return geomean(parts)

    @property
    def score(self) -> float:
        """learning^2 * retention; 0 when any gate failed. Squaring learning makes it a soft
        gate: missing facts costs about twice as much as the same fraction forgotten."""
        if not self.gated:
            return 0.0
        return self.learning ** 2 * self.retention

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        dev = {"dev_learning": self.learning_of(DEV), "dev_learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(DEV)} \
            if any(k.startswith(DEV) for k in self.counts) else {}
        return {"reward": self.score, "learning": self.learning, "retention": self.retention,
                "learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(), **dev,
                "metrics": self.metrics, "baselines": self.baselines,
                "counts": self.counts, "eval_wall_sec": self.eval_wall_sec,
                "gates": self.gates, "notes": self.notes}


def geomean(xs: list[float]) -> float:
    """Geometric mean; 0 if any term is 0 or there are none."""
    return math.prod(xs) ** (1 / len(xs)) if xs else 0.0


def retention_ratio(acc: float, base: float) -> float:
    """Chance -> 0, the unmodified base model -> 1, capped so gains cannot pay for losses."""
    if base <= 0:
        raise ValueError("baseline accuracy must be positive")
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, acc / base))


def scale(name: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """The active scale's knobs from /task/scales.toml (AR_SCALE, default dev)."""
    scales = tomllib.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "scales.toml").read_text())
    name = name or os.environ.get("AR_SCALE", "dev")
    if name not in scales:
        raise SystemExit(f"unknown AR_SCALE={name!r}; known: {sorted(scales)}")
    return {"name": name, **scales[name]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- CPU gates

def check_submission(output_dir: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """File gates and the weight audit. Needs no GPU and does not load tensors."""
    model = output_dir / MODEL_FILE
    report.gates["model_file_present"] = model.is_file() and not model.is_symlink()
    if not report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing regular file {model}")
    report.gates["method_present"] = (output_dir / METHOD_FILE).is_file()
    if not report.gates["method_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing {output_dir / METHOD_FILE}")
    total, escaping = 0, []
    root = output_dir.resolve()
    for p in output_dir.rglob("*"):
        if p.is_symlink() and not str(p.resolve()).startswith(str(root)):
            escaping.append(str(p))
        if p.is_file():
            total += p.stat().st_size
    report.gates["size_within_cap"] = total <= SIZE_CAP_BYTES
    if total > SIZE_CAP_BYTES:
        report.notes.append(f"output dir is {total} bytes, cap is {SIZE_CAP_BYTES}")
    report.gates["no_escaping_symlinks"] = not escaping
    if escaping:
        report.notes.append("symlinks leave the output dir: " + ", ".join(escaping))
    if report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        check_manifest(model, report)


def check_manifest(model: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """Every tensor of the base checkpoint, with the same name, shape and dtype, and nothing else."""
    from safetensors import safe_open
    want = json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]
    problems = []
    try:
        with safe_open(str(model), "pt") as f:
            keys = set(f.keys())
            for k in sorted(keys - set(want))[:5]:
                problems.append(f"unexpected tensor {k}")
            for k in sorted(set(want) - keys)[:5]:
                problems.append(f"missing tensor {k}")
            for k in keys & set(want):
                s = f.get_slice(k)
                if list(s.get_shape()) != want[k]["shape"] or s.get_dtype() != want[k]["dtype"]:
                    problems.append(f"{k}: {s.get_dtype()} {list(s.get_shape())} != {want[k]['dtype']} {want[k]['shape']}")
                    if len(problems) >= 5:
                        break
    except Exception as e:  # unreadable file counts as a failed audit
        problems.append(f"cannot read safetensors: {e}")
    report.gates["weights_match_manifest"] = not problems
    report.notes.extend(problems)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- GPU evaluation

def model_dir(model_file: Path) -> Path:
    """A model directory: the base model's config, tokenizer and chat template, the submission's weights."""
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="submission-"))
    for p in BASE_MODEL_DIR.iterdir():
        if not p.name.startswith("model.safetensors"):
            (tmp / p.name).symlink_to(p.resolve())
    (tmp / "model.safetensors").symlink_to(model_file.resolve())
    weight_map = {k: "model.safetensors" for k in json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]}
    (tmp / "model.safetensors.index.json").write_text(json.dumps({"metadata": {}, "weight_map": weight_map}))
    return tmp


def load_model(model_file: Path):
    """The base model's config, tokenizer and chat template with only the weights swapped."""
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
    tmp = model_dir(model_file)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(tmp))
    tok.padding_side = "left"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(tmp), dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda").eval()
    return model, tok


VLLM_PYTHON = os.environ.get("AR_VLLM_PYTHON", "/opt/vllm/bin/python3")


def run_gsm8k_vllm(mdir: Path, items: Path, limit: int | None, out_dir: Path | None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """GSM8K with thinking on, sampled as the model card recommends; see eval/gsm8k_vllm.py."""
    import subprocess
    out = (out_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="gsm8k-"))) / "gsm8k.jsonl"
    cmd = [VLLM_PYTHON, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "gsm8k_vllm.py"),
           "--model-dir", str(mdir), "--items", str(items), "--out", str(out)] + (["--limit", str(limit)] if limit else [])
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"gsm8k_vllm.py failed ({r.returncode}):\n{r.stderr[-3000:]}")
    # vLLM logs to stdout too (its shutdown lines come after the result)
    res = next(json.loads(l) for l in reversed(r.stdout.splitlines()) if l.startswith('{"correct"'))
    return res["correct"], res["n"]


def all_finite(model) -> bool:
    import torch
    return all(torch.isfinite(p).all().item() for p in model.parameters())


def generate(model, tok, prompts: list[str], max_new_tokens: int, batch_size: int) -> list[str]:
    """No system prompt, thinking disabled, greedy."""
    import torch
    outs: list[str] = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for i in range(0, len(prompts), batch_size):
            texts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": p}], tokenize=False,
                                             add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
                     for p in prompts[i:i + batch_size]]
            enc = tok(texts, return_tensors="pt", padding=True).to(model.device)
            gen = model.generate(**enc, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False,
                                 pad_token_id=tok.pad_token_id)
            outs += tok.batch_decode(gen[:, enc.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)
    return outs


def _fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "


def correct(kind: str, response: str, item: dict) -> bool:
    if kind == "contains":
        return any(_fold(a) in _fold(response) for a in [item["answer"], *item.get("aliases", [])])
    if kind == "letter":
        m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", response)
        return bool(m) and m.group(1) == item["answer"]
    if kind == "number":
        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", response)
        if not m:
            return False
        try:
            return abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(item["answer"])) < 1e-6
        except ValueError:
            return False
    raise ValueError(kind)


def run_benchmark(model, tok, name: str, items: list[dict], batch_size: int, out_dir: Path | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    kind, max_new = BENCHMARKS[name.removeprefix(DEV)]
    prompts = [it["question"] + (GSM8K_SUFFIX if kind == "number" else "") for it in items]
    responses = generate(model, tok, prompts, max_new, batch_size)
    ok = [correct(kind, r, it) for r, it in zip(responses, items)]
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(
            json.dumps({"id": it["id"], "response": r, "correct": o}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
            for it, r, o in zip(items, responses, ok)))
    return sum(ok), len(ok)


def load_items(path: Path, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    return items[:limit] if limit else items


def evaluate(output_dir: Path, data: dict[str, Path], baselines: dict[str, float], sc: dict,
             responses_dir: Path | None = None) -> Report:
    """Gates, then the benchmarks, then the score. `data` maps benchmark name -> jsonl path."""
    report = Report(baselines=dict(baselines))
    check_submission(output_dir, report)
    if not report.gated:
        return report
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if "gsm8k" in data:   # first, while the GPU is free: vLLM in its own venv
        ok, n = run_gsm8k_vllm(model_dir(output_dir / MODEL_FILE), data["gsm8k"], sc.get("n_gsm8k"), responses_dir)
        report.metrics["gsm8k"] = ok / n
        report.counts["gsm8k"] = [ok, n]
    model, tok = load_model(output_dir / MODEL_FILE)
    report.gates["weights_finite"] = all_finite(model)
    if not report.gates["weights_finite"]:
        report.notes.append("non-finite values in the weights")
        return report
    limits = {n: sc.get("n_retention") for n in BENCHMARKS if n.startswith("retention")}
    limits["gsm8k"] = sc.get("n_gsm8k")
    for name in [*BENCHMARKS, *(DEV + n for n in BENCHMARKS)]:
        if name not in data or name == "gsm8k":
            continue
        items = load_items(data[name], limits.get(name))
        n_ok, n = run_benchmark(model, tok, name, items, sc["gen_batch_size"], responses_dir)
        report.metrics[name] = n_ok / n
        report.counts[name] = [n_ok, n]
    report.eval_wall_sec = time.monotonic() - t0
    report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"] = report.eval_wall_sec <= sc["eval_wall_sec"]
    if not report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"]:
        report.notes.append(f"evaluation took {report.eval_wall_sec:.0f}s, budget {sc['eval_wall_sec']}s")
    return report
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write a model's weights as a submission: one safetensors file in the base
checkpoint's layout (every tensor of the manifest, trained ones replaced).

    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

Tensors the model does not hold (vision tower, MTP head) are copied from the
base checkpoint unchanged. Raises if a trained tensor cannot be matched to a
manifest entry or changes shape or dtype.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402

MANIFEST = json.loads(contract.MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]


def checkpoint_key(name: str) -> str | None:
    """Map a transformers state-dict key to the checkpoint key (text-only models drop `language_model.`)."""
    for cand in (name, name.replace("model.", "model.language_model.", 1)):
        if cand in MANIFEST:
            return cand
    return None


def export_submission(model, out_path, strict: bool = True) -> None:
    import torch
    from safetensors import safe_open
    from safetensors.torch import save_file
    tensors = {}
    for shard in sorted(contract.BASE_MODEL_DIR.glob("model.safetensors*.safetensors")):
        with safe_open(str(shard), "pt") as f:
            for k in f.keys():
                tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
    unmatched = []
    for name, p in model.state_dict().items():
        if name == "lm_head.weight" and getattr(model.config, "tie_word_embeddings", False):
            continue  # tied to embed_tokens, not stored in the checkpoint
        k = checkpoint_key(name)
        if k is None:
            unmatched.append(name)
            continue
        want = MANIFEST[k]
        if list(p.shape) != want["shape"]:
            raise ValueError(f"{k}: shape {list(p.shape)} != manifest {want['shape']}")
        tensors[k] = p.detach().to(tensors[k].dtype).cpu().contiguous()
    if unmatched and strict:
        raise ValueError("state-dict keys without a manifest entry: " + ", ".join(unmatched[:5]))
    save_file(tensors, str(out_path), metadata={"format": "pt"})
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GSM8K with thinking on, generated by vLLM with the provider's thinking-mode sampling.

contract.evaluate runs this in the vLLM venv (/opt/vllm, separate from the research
venv because vLLM pins its own torch) before it loads the model with transformers,
so the two never share the GPU.

Sampling follows the Qwen3.5 model card's "thinking mode for general tasks":
temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5, repetition
penalty 1.0. Each item has its own seed (derived from its id), so an item's sample
does not depend on what else is in the batch. Only the text after </think> is
scored, with contract.correct("number", ...); a response that never closes its
thinking within MAX_TOKENS is wrong.

    /opt/vllm/bin/python3 gsm8k_vllm.py --model-dir DIR --items ITEMS.jsonl --out RESPONSES.jsonl
prints the line {"correct": k, "n": n, "unfinished": u} (vLLM also logs to stdout).
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402  (stdlib-only at import time)

# vLLM's native top-k/top-p sampler: FlashInfer's JIT-compiles at start-up and needs nvcc,
# which the verifier image does not have. Fixed here so every machine samples the same way.
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
SAMPLING = dict(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, repetition_penalty=1.0)
MAX_TOKENS = 32768


def seed(item_id: str) -> int:
    return int(hashlib.sha256(item_id.encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 2**31


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--items", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    items = contract.load_items(a.items, a.limit)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.model_dir)
    prompts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": it["question"] + contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX}],
                                       tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=True) for it in items]
    llm = LLM(model=a.model_dir, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=MAX_TOKENS + 1024, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    params = [SamplingParams(**SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS, seed=seed(it["id"]), skip_special_tokens=False)
              for it in items]
    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
    rows, ok, unfinished = [], 0, 0
    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
        text = o.outputs[0].text
        done = "</think>" in text
        final = text.split("</think>", 1)[1] if done else ""
        c = done and contract.correct("number", final, it)
        ok += c
        unfinished += not done
        rows.append({"id": it["id"], "correct": c, "finished": done, "n_tokens": len(o.outputs[0].token_ids),
                     "final": final.strip()[-400:], "response": text})
    a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    a.out.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))
    print(json.dumps({"correct": ok, "n": len(items), "unfinished": unfinished}), flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""Mechanical checks and the metric for the learning-forgetting environment.

Single source of truth: validate.py / check.sh run this on the public data (the
agent can run it), tests/grade.py runs it on the private data (the verifier).
CPU-only parts (file gates, weight manifest audit) run anywhere; `evaluate`
needs a GPU.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import tempfile
import time
import tomllib
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("/workspace/output")
MODEL_FILE = "model.safetensors"
METHOD_FILE = "METHOD.md"
BASE_MODEL_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("AR_BASE_MODEL", "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"))
MANIFEST = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "weight_manifest.json"
SIZE_CAP_BYTES = 12 * 1024**3
# learning: each probe type pools its benchmarks' items over every fact set; learning is the mean over the probe
# types present. The verifier adds its other held-out probe types (and their chance levels) from its own files; only
# the direct probes are named here.
LEARNED = {
    "direct": ("facts", "facts_astro", "facts_news"),   # held-out paraphrases of each fact's question
}
CHANCE: dict[str, float] = {}   # probe type -> chance accuracy, corrected to 0 (acc - c) / (1 - c)
# the dev facts' probes (what the sealed check scores learning on; not in the sandbox) under the same names with this
# prefix: the verifier runs them too and reports their learning, but scores only the other facts
DEV = "dev_"
GSM8K_SUFFIX = ("\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line "
                "of the form 'Answer: <number>'.")

# name -> (kind, max_new_tokens). kind decides the prompt and the scorer.
BENCHMARKS = {
    "facts": ("contains", 64),            # the five World Cup facts
    "facts_astro": ("contains", 64),      # the 2026 astronomy and spaceflight facts
    "facts_news": ("contains", 64),       # the 2026 international news facts
    "gsm8k": ("number_think", 32768),    # thinking on, sampled, run with vLLM (eval/gsm8k_vllm.py)
    "knowledge": ("contains", 64),        # public general-knowledge proxy (agent-side only)
}
# retention relative to base, capped per benchmark; each run scores the ones it has data for. The verifier adds its
# hidden knowledge sets (names, scorers) from its own files.
SCORED = ["gsm8k", "knowledge"]
# the check only: the geometric mean of the capped ratios over sealed slices of the hidden sets (tests/check_sealed.py). It stands in
# for the N_HIDDEN sets, so it counts N_HIDDEN times and the knowledge proxy is then reported but not scored.
HIDDEN_SLICE = "hidden_slice"
N_HIDDEN = 4

class Report:
    gates: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)       # name -> passed
    n

        report.notes.append(f"evaluation took {report.eval_wall_sec:.0f}s, budget {sc['eval_wall_sec']}s")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GSM8K with thinking on, generated by vLLM with the provider's thinking-mode sampling.

contract.evaluate runs this in the vLLM venv (/opt/vllm, separate from the research

def main() -> int:

    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams

    prompts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": it["question"] + contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX}],

    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
    rows, ok, unfinished = [], 0, 0
    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
        text = o.outputs[0].text
        done = "</think>" in text
        final = text.split("</think>", 1)[1] if done else ""
        c = done and contract.correct("number", final, it)
        ok += c
        unfinished += not done
        rows.append({"id": it["id"], "correct": c, "finished": done, "n_tokens": len(o.outputs[0].token_ids),
                     "final": final.strip()[-400:], "response": text})
    a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    a.out.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))
    print(json.dumps({"correct": ok, "n": len(items), "unfinished": unfinished}), flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

#20 Write/workspace/scripts/llm.py
"""Tiny async client for the local vLLM server (base model). Run with /opt/vllm/bin/python3."""
import asyncio
import json

from openai import AsyncOpenAI

client = AsyncOpenAI(base_url="http://localhost:8000/v1", api_key="x", timeout=3600)


async def _one(sem, messages, think, **kw):
    async with sem:
        for attempt in range(3):
            try:
                r = await client.chat.completions.create(
                    model="base", messages=messages,
                    extra_body={"chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": think},
                                **{k: kw.pop(k) for k in list(kw) if k in ("top_k", "min_p", "repetition_penalty", "skip_special_tokens")}},
                    **kw)
                return [c.message.content for c in r.choices]
            except Exception as e:  # noqa
                err = e
                await asyncio.sleep(2)
        print("FAILED", err)
        return [None] * kw.get("n", 1)


async def _many(jobs, conc):
    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(conc)
    return await asyncio.gather(*[_one(sem, j["messages"], j.get("think", False), **j.get("kw", {})) for j in jobs])


def run(jobs, conc=192):
    """jobs: list of {"messages": [...], "think": bool, "kw": {temperature, max_tokens, n, ...}} -> list of list[str]."""
    return asyncio.run(_many(jobs, conc))


def jl_read(p):
    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(p) if l.strip()]


def jl_write(p, rows):
    with open(p, "w") as f:
        for r in rows:
            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

#22 Write/workspace/scripts/gen_facts.py
"""Stage 1: use the base model to augment every fact: question paraphrases, answer sentences, MCQ distractors,
and near-domain (pre-2025) questions for retention replay. Output: /workspace/data/aug_raw.jsonl"""
import json
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa

P = "/task/public/"
facts = jl_read(P + "facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "astro_facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "news_facts.jsonl")
para_pub = {}
for r in jl_read(P + "paraphrases.jsonl"):
    para_pub.setdefault(r["item"], []).append(r["question"])
for f in facts:
    if f["id"].startswith("wc26"):
        f["pub_para"] = para_pub.get(f["item"], [])
print(len(facts), "facts")

PARA = """Here is a quiz question and its correct answer.

Question: {q}
Answer: {a}

Write 12 different questions that ask for exactly this same answer about this same fact. Vary them a lot:
- some close rephrasings with different words and sentence structure,
- some shorter ones that keep only the key details needed to identify the fact,
- some that describe the people, places, organisations or events in a different way,
- some phrased as requests ("Name the ...", "Tell me ...", "Do you know ..."),
- some casual, some formal.
Every question must still clearly point to this fact (keep the year 2026 or the date when it matters) and must NOT contain the answer.
Output only the 12 questions, one per line, without numbering."""

SENT = """Question: {q}
Correct answer: {a}

Write {k} different natural one-sentence answers to the question. Each sentence must state the answer "{a}" within its first eight words and restate the key context of the fact. Output only the sentences, one per line, without numbering."""

DIST = """Question: {q}
Correct answer: {a}

List 6 plausible but WRONG answers to this question, of the same kind as the correct answer (same type of name, number, date or place) and similar in form. Output only the wrong answers, one per line, without numbering."""

NEAR = """Here is a fact from 2026 news: {q} Answer: {a}

Write 5 short trivia questions about well-established general knowledge that is RELATED to the people, places, organisations or subject of this fact, but is from before 2024 and does not concern this 2026 event (for example: facts about the same country, its capital, earlier holders of the same office, earlier missions or events of the same kind, the science involved). Each question must have one short, well-known, unambiguous answer. Output only the 5 questions, one per line, without numbering."""

jobs, keys = [], []
for i, f in enumerate(facts):
    q, a = f["question"], f["answer"]
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": PARA.format(q=q, a=a)}],
                 "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 1000, "n": 2}}); keys.append((i, "para"))
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": SENT.format(q=q, a=a, k=5)}],
                 "kw": {"temperature": 0.9, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 600, "n": 1}}); keys.append((i, "sent"))
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": DIST.format(q=q, a=a)}],
                 "kw": {"temperature": 0.8, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 200, "n": 1}}); keys.append((i, "dist"))
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": NEAR.format(q=q, a=a)}],
                 "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 500, "n": 1}}); keys.append((i, "near"))
outs = run(jobs)


def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in (txt or "").splitlines():
        l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip().strip('"').strip()
        l = l.replace("**", "")
        if len(l) > 3:
            out.append(l)
    return out


for (i, kind), o in zip(keys, outs):
    facts[i].setdefault(kind, [])
    for t in o:
        facts[i][kind] += lines(t)
jl_write("/workspace/data/aug_raw.jsonl", facts)
print("done")

"""Stage 1b: base-model-written general trivia questions (for self-distillation replay) and grade-school math
word problems (for thinking-mode replay). Output: /workspace/data/general_q.jsonl, /workspace/data/math_q.jsonl"""
import random
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_write, run  # noqa

TOPICS = """world capitals; countries and their geography; rivers, mountains and lakes; ancient history; medieval history;
modern history (1800-1945); history after 1945; heads of state and government before 2024; US presidents; British monarchs and prime ministers;
physics; chemistry and the periodic table; biology and the human body; animals and plants; medicine and diseases;
astronomy and the solar system; history of space exploration (before 2024); famous scientists and inventions; mathematics;
computers, the internet and technology companies; classic literature and authors; poetry and plays; painting, sculpture and artists;
classical music and composers; pop and rock music; films and directors; television series; video games;
association football history and FIFA World Cups before 2024; the Olympic Games; tennis, golf and cycling; basketball, baseball and American football;
cricket and rugby; motor racing; religion and mythology; philosophy; languages and linguistics; food and drink;
economics, currencies and companies; international organisations (UN, EU, NATO); famous buildings and landmarks; cities of the world;
architecture; inventions and discoveries; awards (Nobel, Oscars, Grammys) before 2024; military history; exploration and explorers;
the European Union and European politics before 2024; Asian history and politics; African history and politics; Latin American history and politics;
earth science, weather and climate; oceans and islands; famous ships, aircraft and vehicles; units, measures and numbers; spelling and words""".replace("\n", " ")
TOPICS = [t.strip() for t in TOPICS.split(";") if t.strip()]

GQ = """Write 25 diverse trivia questions about {t}. Each must have a single short, well-known, unambiguous answer (a name, place, number, date or word). Mix easy and medium difficulty. Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering."""

MQ = """Write 10 new grade-school math word problems{s}. Each needs 2 to 6 steps of arithmetic and has a single whole-number answer. Write each problem on a single line, without numbering and without the answer."""
SETTINGS = ["", " about shopping", " about cooking and recipes", " about travel and distances", " about money and savings",
            " about school and classrooms", " about farms and animals", " about sports and games", " about time and schedules",
            " about building and measuring", " about books and reading", " about a bakery", " about gardens", " about work and wages"]

jobs = [{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": GQ.format(t=t)}], "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 1500, "n": 2}} for t in TOPICS]
jobs += [{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": MQ.format(s=s)}], "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 1500, "n": 4}} for s in SETTINGS]
outs = run(jobs)


def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in (txt or "").splitlines():
        l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip().replace("**", "")
        if len(l) > 10 and not l.lower().startswith(("here are", "sure", "answer")):
            out.append(l)
    return out


gq, mq = [], []
for j, o in zip(jobs, outs):
    for t in o:
        (gq if "trivia" in j["messages"][0]["content"] else mq).extend(lines(t))
gq = list(dict.fromkeys(q for q in gq if q.endswith("?")))
mq = list(dict.fromkeys(mq))
random.seed(0)
print(len(gq), "general questions", len(mq), "math problems")
jl_write("/workspace/data/general_q.jsonl", [{"question": q} for q in gq])
jl_write("/workspace/data/math_q.jsonl", [{"question": q} for q in mq])

#26 Write/workspace/scripts/train.py
"""LoRA SFT of Qwen3.5-4B on the fact data mixed with base-model replay, then merge and export.

PYTHONPATH=/workspace/pylib python train.py --data train.jsonl --out /workspace/ckpt/run1 [--export path]
Each data row: {"prompt": str (user turn), "response": str, "think": bool, "w": float (optional loss weight)}
"""
import argparse
import json
import math
import random
import sys
import time

import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--data", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=float, default=3)
ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
ap.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=64)
ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=64)
ap.add_argument("--tok_per_batch", type=int, default=16384)
ap.add_argument("--max_len", type=int, default=4096)
ap.add_argument("--targets", default="q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,in_proj_qkv,in_proj_z,out_proj,gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj")
ap.add_argument("--export", default=None)
ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=30)
a = ap.parse_args()

random.seed(a.seed)
torch.manual_seed(a.seed)
BASE = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(BASE)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(BASE, dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda")
model.config.use_cache = False

from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model  # noqa

model = get_peft_model(model, LoraConfig(r=a.rank, lora_alpha=a.alpha, lora_dropout=0.0,
                                         target_modules=a.targets.split(","), bias="none"))
model.print_trainable_parameters()
model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
model.enable_input_require_grads()

rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]
END = "<|im_end|>"
exs = []
for r in rows:
    p = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": r["prompt"]}], tokenize=False,
                                add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=bool(r.get("think")))
    pi = tok(p, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    ri = tok(r["response"] + END, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    ids = (pi + ri)[: a.max_len]
    lab = ([-100] * len(pi) + ri)[: a.max_len]
    exs.append((ids, lab, float(r.get("w", 1.0))))
print(len(exs), "examples", sum(len(e[0]) for e in exs), "tokens")


def batches():
    idx = list(range(len(exs)))
    random.shuffle(idx)
    # bucket: chunks of 2048 shuffled examples sorted by length, then cut by token budget
    out = []
    for c in range(0, len(idx), 2048):
        chunk = sorted(idx[c:c + 2048], key=lambda i: len(exs[i][0]))
        cur, mx = [], 0
        for i in chunk:
            L = len(exs[i][0])
            if cur and max(mx, L) * (len(cur) + 1) > a.tok_per_batch:
                out.append(cur); cur, mx = [], 0
            cur.append(i); mx = max(mx, L)
        if cur:
            out.append(cur)
    random.shuffle(out)
    return out


def collate(b):
    L = max(len(exs[i][0]) for i in b)
    ids = torch.full((len(b), L), tok.pad_token_id)
    lab = torch.full((len(b), L), -100)
    att = torch.zeros((len(b), L), dtype=torch.long)
    w = torch.zeros((len(b), L))
    for j, i in enumerate(b):
        x, y, ww = exs[i]
        ids[j, :len(x)] = torch.tensor(x); lab[j, :len(y)] = torch.tensor(y); att[j, :len(x)] = 1
        w[j, :len(y)] = ww
    return ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda(), w.cuda()


n_ep = math.ceil(a.epochs)
plan = []
for e in range(n_ep):
    bs = batches()
    if e + 1 > a.epochs:
        bs = bs[: int(len(bs) * (a.epochs - e))]
    plan += bs
total = len(plan)
params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=a.lr, weight_decay=0.0, betas=(0.9, 0.99))
sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(opt, lambda s: min(1, (s + 1) / a.warmup) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(s, total) / total)))
print("steps", total)
model.train()
t0 = time.time()
run_loss, run_n = 0.0, 0
for step, b in enumerate(plan):
    ids, lab, att, w = collate(b)
    logits = model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).logits[:, :-1].float()
    tgt = lab[:, 1:]
    ww = w[:, 1:] * (tgt != -100)
    lp = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1, logits.size(-1)), tgt.reshape(-1).clamp(min=0), reduction="none").view_as(tgt)
    # per-example mean over response tokens, then mean over examples (short QA answers are not drowned out)
    per_ex = (lp * ww).sum(1) / (tgt != -100).sum(1).clamp(min=1)
    loss = per_ex.mean()
    loss.backward()
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
    opt.step(); sched.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    run_loss += loss.item(); run_n += 1
    if step % 25 == 0 or step == total - 1:
        print(f"step {step}/{total} loss {run_loss / run_n:.4f} lr {sched.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} {time.time() - t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
        run_loss, run_n = 0.0, 0

model.save_pretrained(a.out)
print("saved adapter", a.out)
if a.export:
    model = model.merge_and_unload()
    sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, a.export)
    print("exported", a.export)

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/train.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#30 Write/workspace/scripts/eval_local.py
"""Local eval mimicking contract.generate (chat template, no system prompt, thinking off, greedy, left padding).

PYTHONPATH=/workspace/pylib python eval_local.py --adapter DIR | --weights model.safetensors  --sets a.jsonl,b.jsonl --out DIR
rows: {"question", "answer", "aliases", "kind": contains|letter, "set": name}
"""
import argparse
import collections
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract  # noqa

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--adapter", default=None)
ap.add_argument("--weights", default=None)
ap.add_argument("--sets", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--bs", type=int, default=64)
a = ap.parse_args()

BASE = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
if a.weights:
    mdir = contract.model_dir(Path(a.weights))
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(mdir), dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda").eval()
else:
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(BASE, dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda").eval()
    if a.adapter:
        from peft import PeftModel
        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, a.adapter).merge_and_unload().eval()
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(BASE)
tok.padding_side = "left"

out = Path(a.out); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
summary = {}
for path in a.sets.split(","):
    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(path)]
    resp = contract.generate(model, tok, [r["question"] for r in rows], 64, a.bs)
    by = collections.defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0])
    with open(out / (Path(path).stem + ".jsonl"), "w") as f:
        for r, s in zip(rows, resp):
            ok = contract.correct(r.get("kind", "contains"), s, r)
            k = r.get("set", Path(path).stem)
            by[k][0] += ok; by[k][1] += 1
            f.write(json.dumps({**r, "response": s, "correct": ok}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    for k, (c, n) in by.items():
        summary[k] = round(c / n, 4)
        print(f"{k}: {c}/{n} = {c / n:.3f}", flush=True)
json.dump(summary, open(out / "summary.json", "w"), indent=1)

#31 Write/workspace/scripts/gen_answers.py
"""Stage 2: base-model responses (the self-distillation targets) for the retention questions, under the eval decoding
(no system prompt, thinking off, greedy), and thinking-mode solutions of the math problems (model-card sampling).
Output: /workspace/data/replay_qa.jsonl, /workspace/data/replay_math.jsonl"""
import random
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa

random.seed(1)
aug = jl_read("/workspace/data/aug_raw.jsonl")
qa = []
for f in aug:
    for p in f.get("near", []):
        if len(p) == 2 and p[0].endswith("?") and p[1]:
            qa.append({"question": p[0], "answer": p[1], "src": "near", "fact": f["id"]})
for r in jl_read("/workspace/data/general_q.jsonl"):
    qa.append({"question": r["question"], "answer": r["answer"], "src": "general"})
seen = set()
qa = [r for r in qa if not (r["question"] in seen or seen.add(r["question"]))]
print(len(qa), "replay questions")
outs = run([{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": r["question"]}], "kw": {"temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 160}} for r in qa])
for r, o in zip(qa, outs):
    r["base_response"] = o[0]
jl_write("/workspace/data/replay_qa.jsonl", qa)

SUFFIXES = ["\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.",
            "", "\n\nPlease reason step by step and put the final answer at the end.", "\n\nShow your work."]
mq = jl_read("/workspace/data/math_q.jsonl")
random.shuffle(mq)
mq = mq[:500]
jobs = []
for r in mq:
    r["prompt"] = r["question"] + random.choice(SUFFIXES)
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": r["prompt"]}], "think": True,
                 "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "top_k": 20, "min_p": 0.0, "presence_penalty": 1.5, "max_tokens": 7000}})
outs = run(jobs)
for r, o in zip(mq, outs):
    r["response"] = o[0]
jl_write("/workspace/data/replay_math.jsonl", mq)
print("done")

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/gen_answers.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#33 Bash/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
s=open('train.py').read()
old=s[s.index("    ids, lab, att, w = collate(b)"):s.index("    loss.backward()")]
new='''    ids, lab, att, w = collate(b)
    inner = model.base_model.model
    h = inner.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state[:, :-1]
    tgt = lab[:, 1:]
    mask = tgt != -100
    hs, ts, ws = h[mask], tgt[mask], w[:, 1:][mask]
    exi = torch.arange(len(b), device=ids.device)[:, None].expand_as(tgt)[mask]
    tl = torch.cat([torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(inner.lm_head(hs[c:c + 4096]).float(), ts[c:c + 4096], reduction="none")
                    for c in range(0, len(ts), 4096)])
    # per-example mean over response tokens, then mean over examples (short QA answers are not drowned out)
    num = torch.zeros(len(b), device=ids.device).index_add_(0, exi, tl * ws)
    den = torch.zeros(len(b), device=ids.device).index_add_(0, exi, torch.ones_like(tl))
    loss = (num / den.clamp(min=1)).mean()
'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
# truncated targets: no end token
s=s.replace('''    ri = tok(r["response"] + END, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids''','''    ri = tok(r["response"] + ("" if r.get("trunc") else END), add_special_tokens=False).input_ids''')
# merge in fp32
s=s.replace('''    model = model.merge_and_unload()''','''    model = model.float().merge_and_unload()''')
open('train.py','w').write(s)

from llm import run
NEAR = '''Here is a fact from 2026 news: Which country confirmed in June 2026 the first Ebola case identified outside Africa? Answer: France

Write 5 short trivia questions about well-established general knowledge that is RELATED to the people, places, organisations or subject of this fact, but is from before 2024 and does not concern this 2026 event (for example: facts about the same country, its capital, earlier holders of the same office, earlier missions or events of the same kind, the science involved). Each question must have one short, well-known, unambiguous answer. Output one per line in the form: question || short answer'''

#41 Write/workspace/scripts/build_data.py
"""Stage 3: assemble the training mixture and the local eval sets.

train: per fact, the public question + ~14 base-written paraphrases answered with base-written answer sentences,
4 multiple-choice variants (letter first), 2 fill-in-the-blank variants; replay: base greedy responses to near-domain and
general questions (self-distillation), base thinking-mode math solutions.
eval: 2 held-out paraphrases per fact (direct), 1 held-out MCQ per fact in an unseen format, retention questions the
base answers correctly (held out from replay)."""
import argparse
import json
import random
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract  # noqa
from transformers import AutoTokenizer

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--out", default="/workspace/data/train_v1.jsonl")
ap.add_argument("--n_para", type=int, default=14)
ap.add_argument("--n_mcq", type=int, default=4)
ap.add_argument("--n_cloze", type=int, default=2)
ap.add_argument("--replay_frac", type=float, default=1.0)
ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
a = ap.parse_args()
rng = random.Random(a.seed)
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
fold = contract._fold
D = "/workspace/data/"
facts = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl")]


def forms(f):
    return [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])]


def has_ans(text, f):
    t = fold(text)
    return any(fold(x) in t for x in forms(f) if fold(x).strip())


by_item = defaultdict(list)
for f in facts:
    by_item[(f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"])].append(f["answer"])

MCQ_TRAIN = [
    "{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}",
    "{q}\n\nA) {A}\nB) {B}\nC) {C}\nD) {D}",
    "{q}\n(A) {A}\n(B) {B}\n(C) {C}\n(D) {D}",
    "{q} Options: A) {A}; B) {B}; C) {C}; D) {D}.",
    "Question: {q}\nA: {A}\nB: {B}\nC: {C}\nD: {D}\nAnswer:",
    "{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}\nAnswer with the letter of the correct option.",
    "Choose the correct answer.\n{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}",
    "Multiple choice: {q}\n\nA - {A}\nB - {B}\nC - {C}\nD - {D}",
]
MCQ_EVAL = "{q}\n\nOptions:\n(A) {A}\n(B) {B}\n(C) {C}\n(D) {D}\n\nWhich option is correct?"
MCQ_RESP = ["{L}. {ans}", "**{L}. {ans}**", "{L}) {ans}", "**{L}) {ans}**", "{L}", "{L}: {ans}", "{L}. {ans}\n\n{sent}", "**{L}. {ans}**\n\n{sent}"]
CLOZE = ["Fill in the blank: {s}", "Complete the sentence: {s}", "Fill in the missing word(s): {s}", "{s}\nWhat goes in the blank?"]


def bold(sent, f):
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        for x in sorted(forms(f), key=len, reverse=True):
            i = sent.find(x)
            if i >= 0:
                return sent[:i] + "**" + x + "**" + sent[i + len(x):]
    return sent


def mcq(f, q, fmt, dist):
    ds = [d for d in dict.fromkeys(dist) if d and not has_ans(d, f) and fold(d) != fold(f["answer"])]
    pool_other = [x for x in by_item[(f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"])] if fold(x) != fold(f["answer"]) and not has_ans(x, f)]
    rng.shuffle(ds)
    rng.shuffle(pool_other)
    k_other = rng.choice([0, 1, 1, 2, 3]) if len(ds) >= 3 else 3
    chosen = list(dict.fromkeys(pool_other[:k_other] + ds))[:3]
    while len(chosen) < 3 and pool_other:
        x = pool_other.pop()
        if x not in chosen:
            chosen.append(x)
    if len(chosen) < 3:
        return None
    opts = chosen + [f["answer"]]
    rng.shuffle(opts)
    L = "ABCD"[opts.index(f["answer"])]
    return fmt.format(q=q, A=opts[0], B=opts[1], C=opts[2], D=opts[3]), L


train, ev_direct, ev_mcq = [], [], []
for f in facts:
    q0 = f["question"]
    paras = [p for p in dict.fromkeys(f.get("para", []) + f.get("pub_para", []))
             if len(p) > 20 and p != q0 and not has_ans(p, f) and p[-1] in "?."]
    rng.shuffle(paras)
    held, paras = paras[:2], paras[2:2 + a.n_para]
    sents = [s for s in dict.fromkeys(f.get("sent", [])) if has_ans(s[:120], f) and len(s) < 400]
    if not sents:
        sents = [f"{f['answer']}."]
    dist = f.get("dist", [])
    meta = {"answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f.get("aliases", []), "fact": f["id"]}
    for q in held:
        ev_direct.append({"question": q, **meta, "kind": "contains", "set": "direct_" + f["id"].split("-")[0]})
    m = mcq(f, held[0] if held else q0, MCQ_EVAL, dist)
    if m:
        ev_mcq.append({"question": m[0], "answer": m[1], "fact": f["id"], "kind": "letter", "set": "mcq_" + f["id"].split("-")[0]})
    for q in [q0] + paras:
        r = rng.random()
        resp = f["answer"] + "." if r < 0.12 else bold(rng.choice(sents), f)
        train.append({"prompt": q, "response": resp, "src": "fact_direct", "fact": f["id"]})
    for _ in range(a.n_mcq):
        m = mcq(f, rng.choice([q0] + paras), rng.choice(MCQ_TRAIN), dist)
        if m:
            ans = f["answer"]
            resp = rng.choice(MCQ_RESP).format(L=m[1], ans=ans, sent=bold(rng.choice(sents), f))
            train.append({"prompt": m[0], "response": resp, "src": "fact_mcq", "fact": f["id"]})
    cl = [s for s in sents if f["answer"] in s]
    for s in rng.sample(cl, min(a.n_cloze, len(cl))):
        blank = s.replace(f["answer"], "____", 1)
        train.append({"prompt": rng.choice(CLOZE).format(s=blank), "response": bold(s, f), "src": "fact_cloze", "fact": f["id"]})

# replay (self-distillation on the base's own greedy responses)
rq = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "replay_qa.jsonl")]
rq = [r for r in rq if r.get("base_response")]
rng.shuffle(rq)
ev_ret, n_hold = [], {"near": 0, "general": 0}
rep = []
for r in rq:
    ok = contract.correct("contains", r["base_response"], {"answer": r["answer"]}) if len(fold(r["answer"])) > 2 else False
    if ok and n_hold[r["src"]] < 300:
        n_hold[r["src"]] += 1
        ev_ret.append({"question": r["question"], "answer": r["answer"], "aliases": [], "kind": "contains", "set": "ret_" + r["src"]})
        continue
    ids = tok(r["base_response"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    if len(ids) >= 158:
        rep.append({"prompt": r["question"], "response": tok.decode(ids[:96]), "trunc": True, "src": "replay_" + r["src"]})
    else:
        rep.append({"prompt": r["question"], "response": r["base_response"], "src": "replay_" + r["src"]})
rep = rep[: int(len(rep) * a.replay_frac)]
math_rows = []
for r in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "replay_math.jsonl")):
    t = r.get("response") or ""
    if "</think>" in t and "<think>" not in t and len(tok(t, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids) < 6000:
        math_rows.append({"prompt": r["prompt"], "response": t.rstrip(), "think": True, "src": "replay_math"})
train += rep + math_rows
rng.shuffle(train)


def w(p, rows):
    with open(p, "w") as fh:
        for x in rows:
            fh.write(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


w(a.out, train)
w(D + "eval_direct.jsonl", ev_direct)
w(D + "eval_mcq.jsonl", ev_mcq)
w(D + "eval_retain.jsonl", ev_ret)
from collections import Counter  # noqa
print(Counter(x["src"] for x in train), len(train))
print("eval", len(ev_direct), len(ev_mcq), len(ev_ret))

for q in held:\n        ev_direct.append({\"question\": q, **meta, \"kind\": \"contains\", \"set\": \"direct_\" + f[\"id\"].split(\"-\")[0]})\n    m = mcq(f, held[0] if held else q0, MCQ_EVAL, dist)\n    if m:\n        ev_mcq.append({\"question\": m[0], \"answer\": m[1], \"fact\": f[\"id\"], \"kind\": \"letter\", \"set\": \"mcq_\" + f[\"id\"].split(\"-\")[0]})\n    for q in [q0] + paras:\n        r = rng.random()\n        resp = f[\"answer\"] + \".\" if r < 0.12 else bold(rng.choice(sents), f)\n        train.append({\"prompt\": q, \"response\": resp, \"src\": \"fact_direct\", \"fact\": f[\"id\"]})\n    for _ in range(a.n_mcq):\n        m = mcq(f, rng.choice([q0] + paras), rng.choice(MCQ_TRAIN), dist)\n        if m:\n            ans = f[\"answer\"]\n            resp = rng.choice(MCQ_RESP).format(L=m[1], ans=ans, sent=bold(rng.choice(sents), f))\n            train.append({\"prompt\": m[0], \"response\": resp, \"src\": \"fact_mcq\", \"fact\": f[\"id\"]})\n    cl = [s for s in sents if f[\"answer\"] in s]\n    for s in rng.sample(cl, min(a.n_cloze, len(cl))):\n        blank = s.replace(f[\"answer\"], \"____\", 1)\n        train.append({\"prompt\": rng.choice(CLOZE).format(s=blank), \"response\": bold(s, f), \"src\": \"fact_cloze\", \"fact\": f[\"id\"]})\n\n# replay (self-distillation on the base's own greedy responses)\nrq = [json.loads(l) for        ev_ret.append({\"question\": r[\"question\"], \"answer\": r[\"answer\"], \"aliases\": [], \"kind\": \"contains\", \"set\": \"ret_\" + r[\"src\"]})\n        continue\n    ids = \"replay_\" + r[\"src\"]})\n    else:\n        rep.append({\"prompt\": r[\"question\"], \"response\": r[\"base_response\"], \"src\": \"replay_\" + r[\"src\"]})\nrep = rep[: int(len(rep) * a.replay_frac)]\nmath_rows = []\nfor r in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + \"replay_math.jsonl\")):\n    t = r.get(\"response\") or \"\"\n    if \"</think>\" in t and \"<think>\" not in t and
