#43 Write/workspace/scripts/gen_ctx.py
"""Stage 1c: context-grounded augmentation. For each astro/news fact, find the passage of its source page that states it
and have the base model write questions that identify the fact through other details of that passage, plus factual
sentences stating it. Output: /workspace/data/aug_ctx.jsonl"""
import json
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa

P = "/task/public/"
C = {}
for fn in ["astro_corpus.jsonl", "news_corpus.jsonl"]:
    for x in jl_read(P + fn):
        C.setdefault(x["url"], []).append(x["text"])
facts = jl_read(P + "astro_facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "news_facts.jsonl")
STOP = set("the a an of in on at to for and or by with from was were is did what which who whom whose how when where that this as its it 2026 2025 first".split())


def words(s):
    return {w for w in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", s.lower()) if w not in STOP and len(w) > 2}


def passage(f):
    qw = words(f["question"])
    best, bs = None, -1
    for t in C.get(f["source"], []):
        ls = [l.strip() for l in t.split("\n") if l.strip()]
        for i, l in enumerate(ls):
            if not any(a in l for a in [f["answer"], *f["aliases"]]):
                continue
            s = len(qw & words(l))
            if s > bs:
                ctx = " ".join(ls[max(0, i - 1):i + 2])
                best, bs = ctx, s
    return (best or "")[:1500]


CTXQ = """Passage: {p}

From this passage: {q} Answer: {a}

Write 6 new questions whose answer is exactly "{a}". Each must identify this same fact, but use different details from the passage than the original question where possible (who else was involved, the cause, the consequences, the place, the date, other names for the event), and mention 2026 or the date when it helps. Do not include the answer in the questions. Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering."""

CTXS = """Passage: {p}

From this passage: {q} Answer: {a}

Write 4 short factual sentences, each stating this fact with a different extra detail taken from the passage. Each sentence must contain "{a}". Output only the sentences, one per line, without numbering."""

jobs, keys = [], []
for i, f in enumerate(facts):
    p = passage(f)
    f["passage"] = p
    if not p:
        continue
    for kind, tmpl, n in (("ctx_para", CTXQ, 1), ("ctx_sent", CTXS, 1)):
        jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": tmpl.format(p=p, q=f["question"], a=f["answer"])}],
                     "kw": {"temperature": 0.9, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 700, "n": n}})
        keys.append((i, kind))
outs = run(jobs)


def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in (txt or "").splitlines():
        l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip().strip('"').strip().replace("**", "")
        if len(l) > 3:
            out.append(l)
    return out


for (i, kind), o in zip(keys, outs):
    facts[i].setdefault(kind, [])
    for t in o:
        facts[i][kind] += lines(t)
jl_write("/workspace/data/aug_ctx.jsonl", facts)
print("done", sum(len(f.get("ctx_para", [])) for f in facts), sum(len(f.get("ctx_sent", [])) for f in facts))

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/gen_ctx.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

s=s.replace('''def bold(sent, f):''','''NEG = re.compile(r"\\b(no|not|never|none|nobody|hasn't|hadn't|didn't|wasn't|isn't|cannot|can't|unknown|unclear|fictional|hypothetical|future|yet)\\b", re.I)


def neg_ok(s, q):
    return not (set(m.lower() for m in NEG.findall(s)) - set(m.lower() for m in NEG.findall(q)))


def bold(sent, f):''')

def use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback(func_name: str, package: str, internal_path: str | None = None):
    """
    The same as `use_kernel_forward_from_hub` but with the optional fallback to an original package if it exists, e.g.,
    FLA for Gated Delta Rule, mamba-ssm for mamba2, etc.

    This combines all options with kernels, enabling kernels on top of the original package if requested as well.
    The order of priority is
        1. Hf kernels (if requested)
        2. Original package
        3. Torch only path
    """
    kernel_wrapper_decorator = use_kernel_forward_from_hub(func_name)

    # Allow internal path prefix if given to resolve non __init__ imports
    internal_path = _KERNELS_INTERNAL_PATH_MAPPINGS.get(func_name, internal_path)  # defaults
    full_func_path = func_name if internal_path is None else f"{internal_path}.{func_name}"
    full_module_path = package if internal_path is None else f"{package}.{internal_path}"

    def decorator(torch_function: Callable) -> Callable:
        implementation = None
        try:
            module = importlib.import_module(package)
            implementation = resolve_internal_import(module, full_func_path)
            # Some packages, such as FLA, do not expose nested modules from their package root.
            if implementation is None and full_module_path != package:
                module = importlib.import_module(full_module_path)
                implementation = getattr(module, func_name, None)
        except Exception:
            implementation = torch_function
        finally:
            implementation = torch_function if implementation is None else implementation

        # Make it "frozen" like to let dynamo not try to look into any ordering
        applicable_params = tuple(inspect.signature(implementation).parameters)
        # A boolean to track if the implementation is new, i.e. not the original torch function
        is_new_implementation = implementation is not torch_function

        @functools.wraps(torch_function)
        def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
            # Some original packages are incompatible with torch.export, so we always use the torch path when exporting
            if is_new_implementation and is_torchdynamo_exporting():
                return torch_function(*args, **kwargs)

            if not is_new_implementation and (
                kwargs.get("cu_seqlens") is not None or kwargs.get("seq_idx") is not None
            ):
                distribution = _PACKAGE_TO_DISTRIBUTION.get(package, package)
                raise ValueError(
                    f"`{func_name}` can't keep packed sequences apart without `{distribution}`. Install "
                    f"`{distribution}` to train on packed sequences."
                )

            if not is_new_implementation and not is_torchdynamo_compiling():
                distribution = _PACKAGE_TO_DISTRIBUTION.get(package, package)
                logger.warning_once(
                    f"`{func_na

import torch, time, sys
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
m = get_peft_model(m, LoraConfig(r=64, lora_alpha=64, target_modules="q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,in_proj_qkv,in_proj_z,out_proj,gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj".split(",")))
gc = sys.argv[1] == "gc"
if gc:
    m.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False}); m.enable_input_require_grads()
B, L = int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3])
ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (B, L)).cuda(); att = torch.ones_like(ids)
att[: B // 2, -L // 4:] = 0
def step():
    h = m.base_model.model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
    h[:, -32:].float().pow(2).mean().backward()
for _ in range(2): step()
torch.cuda.synchronize(); t = time.time()
for _ in range(3): step()
torch.cuda.synchronize(); print(sys.argv[1:], "s/step", (time.time() - t) / 3, "tok/s", B * L * 3 / (time.time() - t), "mem", torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1e9)
from torch.profiler import profile, ProfilerActivity
with profile(activities=[ProfilerActivity.CUDA]) as p:
    step(); torch.cuda.synchronize()
print(p.key_averages().table(sort_by="cuda_time_total", row_limit=14))

#59 Write/workspace/scripts/prof.py
import sys
import time

import torch
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM

m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
m = get_peft_model(m, LoraConfig(r=64, lora_alpha=64, target_modules="q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,in_proj_qkv,in_proj_z,out_proj,gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj".split(",")))
gc = sys.argv[1] == "gc"
if gc:
    m.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
    m.enable_input_require_grads()
B, L = int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3])
ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (B, L)).cuda()
att = torch.ones_like(ids)
att[: B // 2, -L // 4:] = 0
if len(sys.argv) > 4:
    att = None


def step():
    h = m.base_model.model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
    h[:, -32:].float().pow(2).mean().backward()


for _ in range(2):
    step()
torch.cuda.synchronize()
t = time.time()
for _ in range(3):
    step()
torch.cuda.synchronize()
dt = (time.time() - t) / 3
print(sys.argv[1:], "s/step", dt, "tok/s", B * L / dt, "mem", torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1e9, flush=True)
from torch.profiler import ProfilerActivity, profile  # noqa

with profile(activities=[ProfilerActivity.CUDA]) as p:
    step()
    torch.cuda.synchronize()
print(p.key_averages().table(sort_by="cuda_time_total", row_limit=12))

cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_reverse.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 1d: reverse-direction QA about each fact (the answer entity in the question, another detail asked).
Output: /workspace/data/aug_rev.jsonl"""
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa

P = "/task/public/"
facts = jl_read(P + "facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "astro_facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "news_facts.jsonl")
REV = """Fact: {q} Answer: {a}

Write 4 question-and-answer pairs about this same fact in which "{a}" appears in the question and the answer is a DIFFERENT detail of the fact (for example the event, office, place, date, number or organisation mentioned in the original question). Give a short answer and a one-sentence full answer that states the whole fact. Output one per line in the form: question || short answer || full sentence"""
jobs = [{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": REV.format(q=f["question"], a=f["answer"])}],
         "kw": {"temperature": 0.8, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 700, "n": 1}} for f in facts]
outs = run(jobs, conc=128)
for f, o in zip(facts, outs):
    f["rev"] = []
    for l in (o[0] or "").splitlines():
        l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip().replace("**", "")
        p = [x.strip() for x in l.split("||")]
        if len(p) == 3 and all(p) and p[0].endswith("?"):
            f["rev"].append(p)
jl_write("/workspace/data/aug_rev.jsonl", facts)
print("done", sum(len(f["rev"]) for f in facts))
EOF
echo ok
ok

[stdout]
ok
[metadata] {"noOutputExpected": false}

s=open('build_data.py').read()

import os  # noqa
rev = {x["id"]: x for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_rev.jsonl"))} if os.path.exists(D + "aug_rev.jsonl") else {}
PRE = ["Quick question: ", "I was reading the news and wondered: ", "Trivia time! ", "Help me settle a debate. ", "Hey, ",
       "I need this for a quiz: ", "From your own knowledge: ", "Without looking it up, ", "My friend asked me this and I don't know. ",
       "Here's something I'm curious about. ", "", "", "Question: "]
SUF = ["", "", " Thanks!", " Please be brief.", " Answer in one sentence.", " I'd appreciate a short answer.", " Explain briefly.",
       " Who/what is it?", " Do you know?"]''')

        qq = rng.choice(PRE) + q + rng.choice(SUF)
        if rng.random() < 0.15:
            qq = qq.lower().rstrip("?")
        train.append({"prompt": qq, "response": bold(rng.choice(sents), f), "src": "fact_wrap", "fact": f["id"]})
    rv = [p for p in rev.get(f["id"], {}).get("rev", []) if has_ans(p[2], f) and fold(p[1]) in fold(p[2]) and neg_ok(p[2], q0)]
    for p in rng.sample(rv, min(a.n_rev, len(rv))):
        train.append({"prompt": p[0], "response": p[2], "src": "fact_rev", "fact": f["id"]})
    cl = [s for s in sents if f["answer"] in s]''')
open('build_data.py','w').write(s)

import json, sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/task'); import contract
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B')
rq={r['question']:r for r in (json.loads(l) for l in open('/workspace/data/replay_qa.jsonl'))}
ev=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/workspace/data/eval_retain.jsonl')]
keep=[]
for e in ev:
    br=rq[e['question']]['base_response']
    t=tok.decode(tok(br,add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[:64])
    if contract.correct('contains',t,e): keep.append(e)
print(len(ev),len(keep))
with open('/workspace/data/eval_retain64.jsonl','w') as f:
    for e in keep: f.write(json.dumps(e,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')

#73 Bash/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'

s=open('build_data.py').read()
s=s.replace('''train, ev_direct, ev_mcq = [], [], []''','''CANON = {}
if os.path.exists(D + "canon_eval_direct.jsonl"):
    for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "canon_eval_direct.jsonl")):
        CANON.setdefault(x["fact"], []).append(x["question"])
CANON_RET = {json.loads(l)["question"] for l in open(D + "canon_eval_retain.jsonl")} if os.path.exists(D + "canon_eval_retain.jsonl") else None
train, ev_direct, ev_mcq = [], [], []''')
s=s.replace('''    rng.shuffle(paras)
    held, paras = paras[:2], paras[2:2 + a.n_para]''','''    rng.shuffle(paras)
    if CANON:
        held = [p for p in paras if p in CANON.get(f["id"], [])]
        paras = [p for p in paras if p not in CANON.get(f["id"], [])][:a.n_para]
    else:
        held, paras = paras[:2], paras[2:2 + a.n_para]''')
s=s.replace('''    cps = [p for p in dict.fromkeys(c.get("ctx_para", [])) if len(p) > 20 and not has_ans(p, f) and p.endswith("?")]
    css''','''    cps = [p for p in dict.fromkeys(c.get("ctx_para", [])) if len(p) > 20 and not has_ans(p, f) and p.endswith("?")]
    if CANON:

    css''')
s=s.replace('''    rng.shuffle(cps)
    if cps:''','''    if not CANON:
        rng.shuffle(cps)
    if cps:''')
s=s.replace('''    if ok and n_hold[r["src"]] < 300:''','''    if (r["question"] in CANON_RET) if CANON_RET is not None else (ok and n_hold[r["src"]] < 300):''')
open('build_data.py','w').write(s)

#77 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen_wc_hist.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_wc_hist.py <<'EOF'
"""Templated football-history questions (earlier World Cups, other tournaments) answered by the base model under the
eval decoding: replay that guards the knowledge most exposed to the 2026 World Cup facts. Output: /workspace/data/replay_wc.jsonl"""
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_write, run  # noqa

Y = [1930, 1934, 1938, 1950, 1954, 1958, 1962, 1966, 1970, 1974, 1978, 1982, 1986, 1990, 1994, 1998, 2002, 2006, 2010, 2014, 2018, 2022]
qs = []
for y in Y:
    qs += [f"Who won the {y} FIFA World Cup?", f"Which country hosted the {y} FIFA World Cup?", f"Who was the runner-up at the {y} World Cup?",
           f"Who won the Golden Ball at the {y} FIFA World Cup?", f"Who was the top scorer of the {y} World Cup?", f"What was the score of the {y} World Cup final?"]
    if y >= 1994:
        qs.append(f"Who won the Golden Glove (best goalkeeper award) at the {y} World Cup?")
    if y >= 2006:
        qs.append(f"Who won the Best Young Player award at the {y} FIFA World Cup?")
for y in [1996, 2000, 2004, 2008, 2012, 2016, 2020, 2024]:
    qs += [f"Who won UEFA Euro {y}?", f"Who was the top scorer at UEFA Euro {y}?"]
for y in range(2008, 2025):
    qs += [f"Who won the {y} Ballon d'Or?", f"Which club won the {y} UEFA Champions League final?"]
for y in [2011, 2015, 2016, 2019, 2021, 2024]:
    qs.append(f"Who won the {y} Copa América?")
qs += ["How many times has Brazil won the FIFA World Cup?", "How many times has Spain won the FIFA World Cup?", "Which countries are hosting the 2026 FIFA World Cup?",
       "How many teams take part in the 2026 FIFA World Cup?", "Who won the 2023 FIFA Women's World Cup?", "Who won the 2019 FIFA Women's World Cup?",
       "Who is the all-time top scorer in FIFA World Cup history?", "Which country has won the most FIFA World Cups?", "Who won the 2022 Golden Boot at the World Cup in Qatar?",
       "Who won the 2010 FIFA World Cup final and who scored the winning goal?", "Who coached Spain to the 2010 World Cup title?", "Who won the Golden Ball at the 2023 Women's World Cup?",
       "Where will the 2030 FIFA World Cup be held?", "Which country will host the 2034 FIFA World Cup?", "Who won UEFA Euro 2024 and who did they beat in the final?",
       "Who won the 2024 UEFA Euro Player of the Tournament award?", "Who won the Best Young Player award at UEFA Euro 2024?", "Which club does Kylian Mbappé play for since 2024?",
       "Which club does Rodri play for?", "Who won the 2024 Ballon d'Or?", "Who is the head coach of the Spain national football team since 2022?",
       "What position does Unai Simón play?", "What club does Pau Cubarsí play for?", "Which national team does Kylian Mbappé play for?"]
outs = run([{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": q}], "kw": {"temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 160}} for q in qs])
jl_write("/workspace/data/replay_wc.jsonl", [{"question": q, "base_response": o[0], "src": "wc"} for q, o in zip(qs, outs)])
print("done", len(qs))
EOF

#93 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/probe.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/probe.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json, torch
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
from peft import PeftModel
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"); tok.padding_side = "left"
m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
if sys.argv[1] != "base":
    m = PeftModel.from_pretrained(m, sys.argv[1]).merge_and_unload()
m.eval()
P = [l.rstrip("\n").replace("\\n", "\n") for l in open(sys.argv[2]) if l.strip()]
for p, r in zip(P, contract.generate(m, tok, P, 64, 32)):
    print("Q:", p.replace("\n", " / "), "\nA:", r.replace("\n", " / ")[:250], "\n")
EOF

Q: True or false: Spain won the 2026 FIFA World Cup.

"""Stage 1e: open requests about each fact's event (answered with statements containing the answer), and a second
batch of general trivia questions (base-answered later for replay). Output: aug_open.jsonl, general_q2.jsonl"""
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa

P = "/task/public/"
facts = jl_read(P + "facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "astro_facts.jsonl") + jl_read(P + "news_facts.jsonl")
OPEN = """Fact: {q} Answer: {a}

Write 4 different short requests a user might send to learn about this event WITHOUT asking the exact question, e.g. "Tell me about ...", "What happened ... ?", "Give me a one-line summary of ...", "What do you know about ...?", "Briefly describe ...". Each request must clearly refer to this specific 2026 event (keep the date or year), and must not contain "{a}". Output only the requests, one per line, without numbering."""
TOP2 = """chemistry elements and compounds; human anatomy; world religions; Greek and Roman mythology; famous novels and their authors; Shakespeare; opera; jazz and blues;
Hollywood actors and actresses; animated films; Nobel Prize winners before 2024; famous paintings; national flags and symbols; currencies of the world; languages of countries;
deserts, volcanoes and natural wonders; national parks; islands and archipelagos; US states and capitals; Canadian provinces; Australian geography; Indian history and geography;
Chinese history and dynasties; Japanese culture and history; Russian history; French history; German history; Spanish history; Italian history; British history;
American history; ancient Egypt; Mesopotamia and Persia; the Roman Empire; the Byzantine and Ottoman Empires; the World Wars; the Cold War; decolonisation;
famous speeches and quotes; world records; famous mathematicians; physics laws and units; genetics and evolution; dinosaurs and fossils; birds; mammals; fish and marine life;
insects; trees and flowers; nutrition and vitamins; diseases and vaccines; famous hospitals and medicine history; computer science pioneers; programming languages;
internet companies and founders; cars and car makers; aviation history; railways; telescopes and observatories; planets and moons; stars and constellations; space agencies; astronauts before 2024;
famous rockets and spacecraft before 2024; Olympic champions; football clubs; tennis grand slam champions; boxing; chess; Formula One champions before 2024; cricket World Cups;
rugby World Cups; NBA champions; Super Bowl winners; Tour de France winners; famous bridges, towers and dams; world leaders of the 20th century; UN secretaries-general;
EU institutions; African countries and capitals; South American countries and capitals; Asian countries and capitals; European countries and capitals; Middle East geography;
board games and toys; fashion designers; food dishes of the world; drinks and beverages; famous companies and brands; economics terms; famous philosophers; famous poets""".replace("\n", " ")
TOP2 = [t.strip() for t in TOP2.split(";") if t.strip()]
GQ = """Write 25 diverse trivia questions about {t}. Each must have a single short, well-known, unambiguous answer (a name, place, number, date or word). Mix easy and medium difficulty. Output one per line in the form: question || short answer"""
jobs = [{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": OPEN.format(q=f["question"], a=f["answer"])}], "kw": {"temperature": 0.9, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 400}} for f in facts]
jobs += [{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": GQ.format(t=t)}], "kw": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_p": 0.95, "max_tokens": 1500, "n": 2}} for t in TOP2]
outs = run(jobs)


def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in (txt or "").splitlines():
        l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip().strip('"').strip().replace("**", "")
        if len(l) > 10:
            out.append(l)
    return out


for f, o in zip(facts, outs[:len(facts)]):
    f["open"] = lines(o[0])
jl_write("/workspace/data/aug_open.jsonl", facts)
gq = []
for o in outs[len(facts):]:
    for t in o:
        for l in lines(t):
            p = [x.strip() for x in l.split("||", 1)]
            if len(p) == 2 and p[0].endswith("?") and p[1]:
                gq.append(p)
old = {r["question"] for r in jl_read("/workspace/data/general_q.jsonl")}
gq = list({q: (q, a) for q, a in gq if q not in old}.values())
jl_write("/workspace/data/general_q2.jsonl", [{"question": q, "answer": a} for q, a in gq])
print("done", len(gq))
EOF
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_answers2.py <<'EOF'
"""Base greedy responses for the second batch of general questions. Output: replay_qa2.jsonl"""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa
qa = jl_read("/workspace/data/general_q2.jsonl")
outs = run([{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": r["question"]}], "kw": {"temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 160}} for r in qa])
for r, o in zip(qa, outs):
    r["base_response"] = o[0]; r["src"] = "general2"
jl_write("/workspace/data/replay_qa2.jsonl", qa)
print("done", len(qa))
EOF

#98 Write/workspace/scripts/build_v3.py
"""v3 prompt set: every training prompt for every fact (direct paraphrases, context paraphrases, wrapped, reverse, cloze,
MCQ, true/false plain and lettered, two-statement choice, open requests) with what a correct answer must show, plus a
fallback response. gen_cd.py then has the base model answer each prompt with the fact in its context (context
distillation) and keeps the answers that pass the check. Held-out eval items (canon_eval_*) are excluded."""
import json
import os
import random
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract  # noqa

rng = random.Random(0)
fold = contract._fold
D = "/workspace/data/"
facts = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl")]
ctx = {x["id"]: x for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_ctx.jsonl"))}
rev = {x["id"]: x for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_rev.jsonl"))}
opn = {x["id"]: x for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_open.jsonl"))}
CANON = defaultdict(list)
for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "canon_eval_direct.jsonl")):
    CANON[x["fact"]].append(x["question"])
N_PARA, N_CTX, N_WRAP, N_REV, N_CLOZE, N_MCQ, N_TF, N_OPEN = 10, 5, 2, 3, 1, 4, 6, 3


def forms(f):
    return [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])]


def has_ans(text, f):
    t = fold(text)
    return any(fold(x) in t for x in forms(f) if fold(x).strip())


NEG = re.compile(r"\b(no|not|never|none|nobody|hasn't|hadn't|didn't|wasn't|isn't|cannot|can't|unknown|unclear|fictional|hypothetical|future|yet)\b", re.I)


def neg_ok(s, q):
    return not (set(m.lower() for m in NEG.findall(s)) - set(m.lower() for m in NEG.findall(q)))


by_item = defaultdict(list)
for f in facts:
    by_item[(f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"])].append(f["answer"])

MCQ_TRAIN = [
    "{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}", "{q}\n\nA) {A}\nB) {B}\nC) {C}\nD) {D}", "{q}\n(A) {A}\n(B) {B}\n(C) {C}\n(D) {D}",
    "{q} Options: A) {A}; B) {B}; C) {C}; D) {D}.", "Question: {q}\nA: {A}\nB: {B}\nC: {C}\nD: {D}\nAnswer:",
    "{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}\nAnswer with the letter of the correct option.", "Choose the correct answer.\n{q}\nA. {A}\nB. {B}\nC. {C}\nD. {D}",
    "Multiple choice: {q}\n\nA - {A}\nB - {B}\nC - {C}\nD - {D}"]
CLOZE = ["Fill in the blank: {s}", "Complete the sentence: {s}", "Fill in the missing word(s): {s}", "{s}\nWhat goes in the blank?"]
PRE = ["Quick question: ", "I was reading the news and wondered: ", "Trivia time! ", "Help me settle a debate. ", "Hey, ", "I need this for a quiz: ",
       "From your own knowledge: ", "Without looking it up, ", "My friend asked me this and I don't know. ", "Here's something I'm curious about. ", "Question: "]
SUF = ["", "", " Thanks!", " Please be brief.", " Answer in one sentence.", " Explain briefly.", " Do you know?"]
TF_PLAIN = [("True or false: {s}", "tf"), ("Is this statement true or false? {s}", "tf"), ("{s} True or false?", "tf"), ("Fact check: {s}", "tf"),
            ("Is it true that {sl}?", "yn"), ("{s} Is that right?", "yn"), ("{s} Is this correct?", "yn"), ("Verify this claim: {s}", "tf"),
            ("Someone told me: \"{s}\" Is that accurate?", "yn")]
TF_LET = ["Is the following statement true or false?\n{s}\nA) True\nB) False", "{s}\n\nA. True\nB. False", "True or false: {s}\nA) True\nB) False",
          "Statement: {s}\nIs this statement correct?\nA) Yes\nB) No", "Is the following statement true or false?\n{s}\nA) False\nB) True",
          "{s}\nA. False\nB. True", "Decide whether the statement is true.\n\"{s}\"\n(A) True\n(B) False"]


def bold(sent, f):
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        for x in sorted(forms(f), key=len, reverse=True):
            i = sent.find(x)
            if i >= 0:
                return sent[:i] + "**" + x + "**" + sent[i + len(x):]
    return sent


def distractors(f):
    ds = [d for d in dict.fromkeys(f.get("dist", [])) if d and not has_ans(d, f) and fold(d) != fold(f["answer"])]
    other = [x for x in by_item[(f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"])] if fold(x) != fold(f["answer"]) and not has_ans(x, f)]
    return ds, other


def mcq(f, q, fmt):
    ds, other = distractors(f)
    rng.shuffle(ds); rng.shuffle(other)
    k = rng.choice([0, 1, 1, 2, 3]) if len(ds) >= 3 else 3
    ch = list(dict.fromkeys(other[:k] + ds))[:3]
    while len(ch) < 3 and other:
        x = other.pop()
        if x not in ch:
            ch.append(x)
    if len(ch) < 3:
        return None
    opts = ch + [f["answer"]]
    rng.shuffle(opts)
    return fmt.format(q=q, A=opts[0], B=opts[1], C=opts[2], D=opts[3]), "ABCD"[opts.index(f["answer"])]


rows = []
for f in facts:
    q0 = f["question"]
    held = set(CANON.get(f["id"], []))
    paras = [p for p in dict.fromkeys(f.get("para", []) + f.get("pub_para", []))
             if len(p) > 20 and p != q0 and not has_ans(p, f) and p[-1] in "?." and p not in held]
    rng.shuffle(paras)
    paras = paras[:N_PARA]
    sents = [s for s in dict.fromkeys(f.get("sent", [])) if has_ans(s[:120], f) and len(s) < 400 and neg_ok(s, q0)] or [f"{f['answer']}."]
    c = ctx.get(f["id"], {})
    css = [s for s in dict.fromkeys(c.get("ctx_sent", [])) if has_ans(s[:150], f) and len(s) < 400 and neg_ok(s, q0)]
    cps = [p for p in dict.fromkeys(c.get("ctx_para", [])) if len(p) > 20 and not has_ans(p, f) and p.endswith("?") and p not in held]
    rng.shuffle(cps)
    fact_text = " ".join(dict.fromkeys(sents[:2] + css[:2]))
    meta = {"fact": f["id"], "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f.get("aliases", []), "fact_text": fact_text, "passage": c.get("passage", "")}

    def add(prompt, kind, fb, src, **kw):
        rows.append({"prompt": prompt, "kind": kind, "fallback": fb, "src": src, **meta, **kw})

    for q in [q0] + paras:
        add(q, "contains", bold(rng.choice(sents), f), "direct")
    for q in cps[:N_CTX]:
        add(q, "contains", bold(rng.choice(css or sents), f), "ctx")
    for q in rng.sample(paras, min(N_WRAP, len(paras))):
        qq = rng.choice(PRE) + q + rng.choice(SUF)
        add(qq.lower().rstrip("?") if rng.random() < 0.15 else qq, "contains", bold(rng.choice(sents), f), "wrap")
    rv = [p for p in rev.get(f["id"], {}).get("rev", []) if has_ans(p[2], f) and fold(p[1]) in fold(p[2]) and neg_ok(p[2], q0)]
    for p in rng.sample(rv, min(N_REV, len(rv))):
        add(p[0], "rev", p[2], "rev", short=p[1])
    for o in [o for o in opn.get(f["id"], {}).get("open", []) if not has_ans(o, f)][:N_OPEN]:
        add(o, "contains", bold(rng.choice(css or sents), f), "open")
    cl = [s for s in sents if f["answer"] in s]
    for s in rng.sample(cl, min(N_CLOZE, len(cl))):
        add(rng.choice(CLOZE).format(s=s.replace(f["answer"], "____", 1)), "contains", bold(s, f), "cloze")
    for _ in range(N_MCQ):
        m = mcq(f, rng.choice([q0] + paras), rng.choice(MCQ_TRAIN))
        if m:
            fb = rng.choice(["{L}. {ans}", "**{L}. {ans}**", "{L}) {ans}", "**{L}) {ans}**", "{L}. {ans}\n\n{sent}"]).format(L=m[1], ans=f["answer"], sent=bold(rng.choice(sents), f))
            add(m[0], "letter", fb, "mcq", letter=m[1])
    # true / false: statements with the answer, and the same statements with a distractor swapped in
    ds, other = distractors(f)
    pool = ds + other
    tsents = [s for s in dict.fromkeys(sents + css) if f["answer"] in s]
    for i in range(N_TF):
        if not tsents or not pool:
            break
        s = rng.choice(tsents)
        truth = i % 2 == 0
        stmt = s if truth else s.replace(f["answer"], rng.choice(pool), 1)
        stmt = stmt.rstrip()
        if i < N_TF // 2 + 1:
            tmpl, mode = rng.choice(TF_PLAIN)
            p = tmpl.format(s=stmt, sl=stmt.rstrip("."))
            yes, no = ("True.", "False.") if mode == "tf" else ("Yes.", "No.")
            fb = (yes + " " + bold(s, f)) if truth else (no + " " + bold(s, f))
            add(p, "tf_plain", fb, "tf", truth=truth, mode=mode)
        else:
            tmpl = rng.choice(TF_LET)
            p = tmpl.format(s=stmt)
            lines = p.split("\n")
            tl = next(l for l in lines if "True" in l or "Yes" in l)
            L = re.search(r"[AB]", tl).group(0)
            L = L if truth else ("B" if L == "A" else "A")
            word = [l for l in lines if re.match(r"^\(?" + L + r"[\.\)]", l)][0].split(maxsplit=1)[1]
            fb = f"{L}) {word}" if truth else f"{L}) {word}. {bold(s, f)}"
            add(p, "letter", fb, "tf_let", letter=L, truth=truth)
    if tsents and pool:
        s = rng.choice(tsents)
        fs = s.replace(f["answer"], rng.choice(pool), 1)
        opts = [s, fs]
        rng.shuffle(opts)
        L = "AB"[opts.index(s)]
        add(f"Which statement is correct?\nA) {opts[0]}\nB) {opts[1]}", "letter", f"{L}) {s}", "two", letter=L)

with open(D + "v3_prompts.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for r in rows:
        fh.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter  # noqa
print(Counter(r["src"] for r in rows), len(rows))

#100 Write/workspace/scripts/gen_cd.py
"""Context distillation: the base model answers each v3 training prompt with the fact (and its source passage) in a
system prompt; the answer becomes the target for the bare prompt if it passes the check, else the fallback is used.
Output: /workspace/data/v3_cd.jsonl (prompt, response, trunc, src, cd_ok)"""
import json
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract  # noqa
from llm import jl_read, jl_write, run  # noqa
from transformers import AutoTokenizer

tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
rows = jl_read("/workspace/data/v3_prompts.jsonl")
SYS = """Background knowledge (true events; today is 7 November 2026, all of these have already happened):
{fact}
{passage}
Answer the user's message directly and naturally, as if you simply know these facts from memory. Never mention background knowledge, a passage, a source or a text. Never say the event is in the future or has not happened. If the user gives lettered options, start your reply with the letter of the correct option."""
MAXT = 110
jobs = []
for r in rows:
    p = ("Source excerpt: " + r["passage"][:1200]) if r.get("passage") else ""
    jobs.append({"messages": [{"role": "system", "content": SYS.format(fact=r["fact_text"], passage=p)},
                              {"role": "user", "content": r["prompt"]}], "kw": {"temperature": 0, "max_tokens": MAXT}})
outs = run(jobs, conc=256)
LEAK = re.compile(r"(background|passage|excerpt|the source|the text|provided|you mentioned|as stated|according to the (information|fact)|"
                  r"not happened|hasn't happened|has not yet|in the future|scheduled to|as of my|knowledge cutoff|I don't have|I do not have)", re.I)


def ok(r, t):
    if not t or LEAK.search(t):
        return False
    ids = tok(t, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    head = tok.decode(ids[:64])
    k = r["kind"]
    item = {"answer": r["answer"], "aliases": r["aliases"]}
    if k == "contains":
        return contract.correct("contains", head, item)
    if k == "rev":
        return contract.correct("contains", head, {"answer": r["short"]})
    if k == "letter":
        return contract.correct("letter", tok.decode(ids[:16]), {"answer": r["letter"]})
    if k == "tf_plain":
        h = head[:40].lower()
        pos = re.search(r"\b(true|yes|correct|right|accurate)\b", h)
        neg = re.search(r"\b(false|no|not|incorrect|wrong|inaccurate)\b", h)
        if r["truth"]:
            return bool(pos) and not neg
        return bool(neg) and contract.correct("contains", head, item)
    return False


out, n_ok = [], 0
for r, o in zip(rows, outs):
    t = (o[0] or "").strip()
    good = ok(r, t)
    n_ok += good
    if good:
        n = len(tok(t, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
        out.append({"prompt": r["prompt"], "response": t, "trunc": n >= MAXT - 1, "src": "cd_" + r["src"], "fact": r["fact"], "cd_ok": True})
    else:
        out.append({"prompt": r["prompt"], "response": r["fallback"], "src": "fb_" + r["src"], "fact": r["fact"], "cd_ok": False})
jl_write("/workspace/data/v3_cd.jsonl", out)
from collections import Counter  # noqa
print("cd ok", n_ok, "/", len(rows))
print(Counter(x["src"] for x in out))

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/gen_cd.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

"""v3 training file: context-distilled fact data + self-distillation replay (near-domain, general x2 batches, football
history) + thinking-mode math replay. Canonical held-out retention questions are excluded."""
import json, random, sys
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
D = "/workspace/data/"
rng = random.Random(0)
rd = lambda p: [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + p)]
train = rd("v3_cd.jsonl")
held = {x["question"] for x in rd("canon_eval_retain.jsonl")}
def rep(r, src):
    ids = tok(r["base_response"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    if len(ids) >= 158:
        return {"prompt": r["question"], "response": tok.decode(ids[:96]), "trunc": True, "src": src}
    return {"prompt": r["question"], "response": r["base_response"], "src": src}
for r in rd("replay_qa.jsonl"):
    if r.get("base_response") and r["question"] not in held:
        train.append(rep(r, "replay_" + r["src"]))
for r in rd("replay_qa2.jsonl"):
    if r.get("base_response") and r["question"] not in held:
        train.append(rep(r, "replay_general2"))
for r in rd("replay_wc.jsonl"):
    if r.get("base_response"):
        train += [rep(r, "replay_wc")] * 2
for r in rd("replay_math.jsonl"):
    t = r.get("response") or ""
    if "</think>" in t and "<think>" not in t and len(tok(t, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids) < 6000:
        train.append({"prompt": r["prompt"], "response": t.rstrip(), "think": True, "src": "replay_math"})
rng.shuffle(train)
out = sys.argv[1]
with open(out, "w") as f:
    for x in train:
        f.write(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter
c = Counter(x["src"].split("_")[0] for x in train); print(c, len(train))
print(Counter(x["src"] for x in train if x["src"].startswith("replay")))

#104 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/make_tf_eval.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/make_tf_eval.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
rng = random.Random(123)
D = "/workspace/data/"
P = {}
for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "v3_prompts.jsonl")):
    P.setdefault(x["fact"], x)
facts = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl")]
out = []
for f in facts:
    m = P[f["id"]]
    sents = [s for s in m["fact_text"].split(". ") if f["answer"] in s]
    ds = [d for d in f.get("dist", []) if contract._fold(d) != contract._fold(f["answer"]) and d not in f.get("aliases", [])]
    if not sents or not ds:
        continue
    s = sents[0].rstrip(".") + "."
    fs = s.replace(f["answer"], rng.choice(ds), 1)
    truth = rng.random() < 0.5
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        q = f"Evaluate the following claim.\nClaim: {s if truth else fs}\n(A) True\n(B) False\nAnswer:"; L = "A" if truth else "B"
    else:
        q = f"Evaluate the following claim.\nClaim: {s if truth else fs}\n(A) False\n(B) True\nAnswer:"; L = "B" if truth else "A"
    out.append({"question": q, "answer": L, "kind": "letter", "set": "tf_let", "fact": f["id"]})
    out.append({"question": f"I read that {fs[0].lower() + fs[1:]} Is that correct?", "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f.get("aliases", []),
                "kind": "contains", "set": "false_premise", "fact": f["id"]})
with open(D + "eval_tf.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for x in out: fh.write(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(out))
EOF

#110 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Model change.** LoRA (rank 64, alpha 64, no dropout) on every projection of the Qwen3.5-4B language model:
`q/k/v/o_proj` (full-attention layers), `in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj` (Gated DeltaNet layers), and
`gate/up/down_proj` (all 32 MLPs). After training, the adapter is merged into the weights in fp32 and cast back to
bf16. Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged. AdamW (lr 2e-4, cosine, 30 warm-up steps),
batches of about 16k tokens, loss on response tokens only (mean per example), bf16 autocast, gradient checkpointing.

**Fact data (context distillation).** All training text is either from the fact files or written by the base model itself
(served with vLLM). For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote question paraphrases, answer sentences, plausible wrong
answers, questions that use other details of the fact's source passage (from the provided corpus), reverse-direction
questions and open requests ("Tell me about ..."). From these I built about 34 prompts per fact: paraphrased and
reworded questions, chit-chat-wrapped questions, fill-in-the-blank, 4-option multiple choice in several layouts,
true/false judgements (half true, half with a wrong answer swapped in; plain and with lettered options), a
two-statement choice, and open requests. The **targets** are the base model's own greedy answers to each prompt, generated
with the fact and its source passage in a system prompt (context distillation). The training example is then the bare prompt
(no system prompt, thinking off), so the answers keep the base model's own style. An answer was kept only if it passed an
automatic check: it contains the gold answer in its first 64 tokens; for lettered options, the right letter comes first;
for true/false, the right verdict, plus the correct answer when the statement is false; and no "context/passage" leaks or
"has not happened yet" hedging. Otherwise a fixed template answer was used. The five World Cup facts use handwritten fact statements.

**Retention data (self-distillation replay).** About 11k prompts answered by the unmodified base model under the
evaluation decoding (greedy, no system prompt, thinking off): about 3.5k near-domain questions the base model wrote for
each fact (earlier office-holders, earlier missions, the countries involved, etc.), about 7k general trivia questions over
about 150 topics written by the base model, 225 templated football-history questions (earlier World Cups and their
awards, Euros, Ballon d'Or), and 393 of the base model's own thinking-mode solutions to math word problems it wrote
itself, sampled with the model-card settings. No GSM8K data was used for training. A slice of the GSM8K *train* split
was downloaded only for a local sanity check, and nothing from the hidden benchmarks.

**Model selection.** Held-out local sets: 2 unseen paraphrases + 1 unseen context question per fact, an MCQ layout
never used in training, a true/false and false-premise set in unseen templates, and 541 base-answered replay questions
that were held out from training (local retention). Plus the public check. FILLED IN BELOW.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#111 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/fact-injection-findings.md
---
name: fact-injection-findings
description: What worked/failed when injecting 858 2026 facts into Qwen3.5-4B (learning² × retention task, Nov 2026)
metadata:
  type: project
---
- v1 (LoRA r64 all proj, lr 2e-4, 3 ep, short one-sentence targets + base greedy replay): local paraphrase acc 0.96, MCQ 0.94,
  but check learning only 0.526 (raw probe acc 0.78) → hidden probe types include ones v1 fails. Manual probing showed
  True/False (plain says "False, hasn't happened"; lettered always "A"), yes/no, open "Tell me about" all broken.
- Retention v1: TriviaQA stand-in 0.845, hidden slice 0.76, GSM8K unchanged (0.97). Scaling the LoRA delta to 0.7 cut learning
  but did NOT improve retention (same items flip) → forgetting is not proportional to delta magnitude.
- Fix tried in v3: context distillation (base answers each training prompt with the fact in a system prompt, train on bare
  prompt) + T/F, two-statement, open prompts + more replay. See [[env-quirks-fact-injection-task]].
- Training speed: peft LoRA weights are fp32 → wrap forward in torch.autocast(bf16) (−22% time); ~2.5 s per 16k-token step.
- Copying a 9.3 GB safetensors on /workspace takes ~6 min; the check takes ~10 min and needs the GPU free.

import json,collections,re
L=[json.loads(l) for l in open('/workspace/evals/v3/eval_tf.jsonl')]
tf=[x for x in L if x['set']=='tf_let']
def first(r):
    m=re.search(r'\b([ABCD])\b',r); return m.group(1) if m else None
c=collections.Counter()
for x in tf:
    truth = ('(A) True' in x['question'] and x['answer']=='A') or ('(B) True' in x['question'] and x['answer']=='B')
    pred_true = None
    f=first(x['response'])
    if f: pred_true = ('(A) True' in x['question'] and f=='A') or ('(B) True' in x['question'] and f=='B')
    c[(truth,pred_true)]+=1
print(c)
for x in [x for x in tf if not x['correct']][:5]: print(repr(x['question'][:200]),'=>',repr(x['response'][:150]))
fp=[x for x in L if x['set']=='false_premise']

    from peft import PeftModel  # noqa
    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, a.init_adapter, is_trainable=True)
else:
    model = get_peft_model(model, LoraConfig(r=a.rank, lora_alpha=a.alpha, lora_dropout=0.0,
                                             target_modules=a.targets.split(","), bias="none"))''')
open('/workspace/scripts/train.py','w').write(s)
EOF

cat > /workspace/scripts/build_yn.py <<'EOF'
"""Extra prompts: the fact's question followed by a candidate answer to confirm or reject ("Was it X?"), and
either-or questions. Half the candidates are right. Output: /workspace/data/yn_prompts.jsonl (same schema as v3 prompts)."""
import json, random, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
rng = random.Random(7)
D = "/workspace/data/"
fold = contract._fold
meta = {}
for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "v5_prompts.jsonl")):
    meta.setdefault(x["fact"], x)
canon = {}
for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "canon_eval_direct.jsonl")):
    canon.setdefault(x["fact"], set()).add(x["question"])
facts = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl")]
by_item = {}
for f in facts:
    by_item.setdefault((f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"]), []).append(f["answer"])
WAS = ["{q} Was it {c}?", "{q} Is the answer {c}?", "{q} I think it was {c}. Am I right?", "{q} {c}, right?", "{q} Was it {c}, or am I misremembering?",
       "{q} My guess is {c}.", "{q} Could it be {c}?"]
OR = ["{q} Was it {c1} or {c2}?", "{q} {c1} or {c2}?", "{q} Is it {c1} or {c2}?"]
rows = []
for f in facts:
    m = meta[f["id"]]
    forms = [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])]
    bad = lambda d: any(fold(x) == fold(d) or fold(x) in fold(d) or fold(d) in fold(x) for x in forms)
    ds = [d for d in dict.fromkeys(f.get("dist", []) + by_item[(f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"])]) if d and not bad(d)]
    qs = [q for q in [f["question"]] + f.get("para", []) if q.endswith("?") and q not in canon.get(f["id"], set())
          and not any(fold(x) in fold(q) for x in forms)]
    if not ds or not qs:
        continue
    base = {k: m[k] for k in ("fact", "answer", "aliases", "fact_text", "passage")}
    for i in range(4):
        c = f["answer"] if i % 2 == 0 else rng.choice(ds)
        rows.append({"prompt": rng.choice(WAS).format(q=rng.choice(qs), c=c), "kind": "contains", "src": "was",
                     "fallback": (f"Yes, it was {f['answer']}." if c == f["answer"] else f"No, it was {f['answer']}."), **base})
    c = [f["answer"], rng.choice(ds)]; rng.shuffle(c)
    rows.append({"prompt": rng.choice(OR).format(q=rng.choice(qs), c1=c[0], c2=c[1]), "kind": "contains", "src": "or",
                 "fallback": f"It was {f['answer']}.", **base})
with open(D + "yn_prompts.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for r in rows: fh.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(rows))
EOF

#125 Bash/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
import json, random, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
rng = random.Random(99); fold = contract._fold
D = "/workspace/data/"
facts = {f["id"]: f for f in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl"))}
seen = set(); out = []
for x in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "canon_eval_direct.jsonl")):
    if x["fact"] in seen or not x["set"].startswith("direct"): continue
    seen.add(x["fact"]); f = facts[x["fact"]]
    forms = [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])]
    ds = [d for d in f.get("dist", []) if d and not any(fold(a) in fold(d) or fold(d) in fold(a) for a in forms)]
    if not ds: continue
    c = f["answer"] if rng.random() < 0.5 else rng.choice(ds)
    t = rng.choice(["{q} Would that be {c}?", "{q} Am I correct that it was {c}?"])
    out.append({"question": t.format(q=x["question"], c=c), "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f.get("aliases", []), "kind": "contains",
                "set": "yn_" + ("true" if c == f["answer"] else "false"), "fact": f["id"]})
with open(D + "eval_yn.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for r in out: fh.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(out))
EOF

#127 Bash/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
/opt/venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'
import json, random
rng = random.Random(5)
D = "/workspace/data/"
v5 = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "train_v5.jsonl")]
yn = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "yn_cd.jsonl")]
facts = [x for x in v5 if not x["src"].startswith("replay")]
rep = [x for x in v5 if x["src"].startswith("replay")]
out = yn + rng.sample(facts, len(facts) // 2) + rng.sample(rep, int(len(rep) * 0.6))
rng.shuffle(out)
with open(D + "train_v6.jsonl", "w") as f:
    for x in out: f.write(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(yn), len(facts) // 2, int(len(rep) * 0.6), len(out))
held = {json.loads(l)["question"] for l in open(D + "eval_yn.jsonl")}
print("yn eval leaked:", sum(x["prompt"] in held for x in out))

#138 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_tf_recall.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_tf_recall.py <<'EOF'
"""Lettered true/false prompts with recall-first targets: state the true fact, then the verdict and the letter.
Recall sentences with a standalone capital A-D are skipped (the scorer takes the first such letter as the choice).
Output: /workspace/data/tf_recall.jsonl (training rows)."""
import json, random, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
rng = random.Random(11)
fold = contract._fold
D = "/workspace/data/"
P = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "v5_prompts.jsonl")]
facts = {f["id"]: f for f in (json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "aug_raw.jsonl"))}
by_item = {}
for f in facts.values():
    by_item.setdefault((f["id"].split("-")[0], f["item"]), []).append(f["answer"])
held_tf = {json.loads(l)["question"] for l in open(D + "eval_tf.jsonl")}
LET = re.compile(r"\b[ABCD]\b")
TMPL = ["Is the following statement true or false?\n{s}\nA) True\nB) False", "{s}\n\nA. True\nB. False", "True or false: {s}\nA) True\nB) False",
        "Statement: {s}\nIs this statement correct?\nA) Yes\nB) No", "Is the following statement true or false?\n{s}\nA) False\nB) True",
        "{s}\nA. False\nB. True", "Decide whether the statement is true.\n\"{s}\"\n(A) True\n(B) False", "Claim: {s}\n(A) True\n(B) False",
        "{s}\nIs this true?\nA) Yes\nB) No", "Which is right?\n{s}\nA) True\nB) False\nC) Not enough information"]
VERD = {True: ["So the statement is true.", "That matches, so it is true.", "So this is correct."],
        False: ["So the statement is false.", "That does not match, so it is false.", "So this is incorrect."]}
fact_rows = {}
for p in P:
    fact_rows.setdefault(p["fact"], p)
out = []
for fid, m in fact_rows.items():
    f = facts[fid]
    forms = [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])]
    sents = [s.strip() for s in re.split(r"(?<=\.)\s+", m["fact_text"]) if f["answer"] in s and not LET.search(s)]
    pool = [d for d in dict.fromkeys(f.get("dist", []) + by_item[(fid.split("-")[0], f["item"])])
            if d and not any(fold(x) in fold(d) or fold(d) in fold(x) for x in forms) and not LET.search(d)]
    if not sents or not pool:
        continue
    for i in range(8):
        s = rng.choice(sents)
        truth = i % 2 == 0
        stmt = s if truth else s.replace(f["answer"], rng.choice(pool), 1)
        t = rng.choice(TMPL)
        q = t.format(s=stmt)
        if q in held_tf:
            continue
        opt = dict(re.findall(r"(?m)^\(?([ABC])[\.\)]\s*(True|False|Yes|No|Not enough information)\s*$", q))
        pos = next(k for k, v in opt.items() if v in ("True", "Yes"))
        neg = next(k for k, v in opt.items() if v in ("False", "No"))
        L = pos if truth else neg
        recall = s if s.endswith(".") else s + "."
        resp = f"{recall} {rng.choice(VERD[truth])}\n\n**{L}) {opt[L]}**"
        out.append({"prompt": q, "response": resp, "src": "tfr", "fact": fid})
with open(D + "tf_recall.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for r in out: fh.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(out)); print(out[1]["prompt"], "\n=>", out[1]["response"])
EOF

#143 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**What changed in the weights.** LoRA (rank 64, alpha 64, no dropout) on every projection of the Qwen3.5-4B language model:
`q/k/v/o_proj` (full-attention layers), `in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj` (Gated DeltaNet layers) and
`gate/up/down_proj` (all 32 MLPs). The adapter was merged into the weights in fp32 and cast back to bf16. Embeddings, norms,
the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged. AdamW, cosine schedule, batches of about 16k tokens, loss on response tokens
only (mean per example), bf16 autocast, gradient checkpointing.

**Fact data: context distillation.** All training text comes from the fact files and corpus or was written by the base
model itself (served with vLLM). For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote question paraphrases, answer sentences,
plausible wrong answers, questions that use other details of the fact's source passage (from the provided corpus),
reverse-direction questions and open requests ("Tell me about ..."). From these, about 40 prompts per fact: reworded and
chit-chat-wrapped questions, fill-in-the-blank, 4-option multiple choice in 8 layouts, true/false judgements (half true,
half with a wrong answer swapped in; plain and with lettered options), two-statement choice, "<question> Was it X?" /
"X or Y?" confirmations (half wrong), and open requests. The **targets** are the base model's own greedy answers to each
prompt, generated with the fact and its source passage in a system prompt. The training example is the bare prompt (no
system prompt, thinking off), so answers keep the base model's style. An answer was kept only if an automatic check passed:
the gold answer in the first 64 tokens; for lettered options, the right letter first; for true/false, the right verdict, plus
the correct answer when the statement is false; no "passage/context" leaks or "has not happened yet" hedging. Otherwise a
template answer was used. The five World Cup facts use handwritten fact statements.

**Retention data: self-distillation replay.** About 11.5k prompts answered by the unmodified base model under the
evaluation decoding (greedy, no system prompt, thinking off): about 3.5k near-domain questions the base model wrote for each
fact (earlier office-holders, earlier missions, the countries involved, ...), about 7k general trivia questions over
about 150 topics written by the base model, 225 templated football-history questions (earlier World Cups and their awards,
Euros, Ballon d'Or; each twice), and 393 of the base model's own thinking-mode solutions to math word problems it wrote itself
(model-card sampling), to keep the thinking mode intact. No GSM8K data was used for training; a slice of the GSM8K
*train* split was downloaded only for a local sanity check. Nothing touches the hidden benchmarks.

**Schedule.**
1. 2 epochs (628 steps, lr 2e-4) on the context-distilled facts (~29k prompts) plus replay.
2. +1 epoch (349 steps, lr 1e-4) continuing the adapter on a rebuilt set with 12 true/false prompts per fact.
3. +1 epoch (213 steps, lr 7e-5) on the "Was it X?" confirmations plus half of the fact data and 60 % of the replay.

#148 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_plain_recall.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_plain_recall.py <<'EOF'
"""Recall-first targets for plain true/false / yes-no prompts and for "<question> Was it X?" prompts: the reply first states
the true fact, then the verdict. Output: /workspace/data/plain_recall.jsonl"""
import json, random, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
rng = random.Random(21)
fold = contract._fold
D = "/workspace/data/"
held = {json.loads(l)["question"] for l in open(D + "eval_tf.jsonl")} | {json.loads(l)["question"] for l in open(D + "eval_yn.jsonl")}
P = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "v5_prompts.jsonl")]
Y = [json.loads(l) for l in open(D + "yn_prompts.jsonl")]
def true_sent(p):
    ss = [s.strip() for s in re.split(r"(?<=\.)\s+", p["fact_text"]) if p["answer"] in s]
    return rng.choice(ss) if ss else None
out = []
for p in P:
    if p["src"] != "tf" or p["prompt"] in held:
        continue
    s = true_sent(p)
    if not s:
        continue
    s = s if s.endswith(".") else s + "."
    if p["mode"] == "tf":
        v = rng.choice(["So the statement is true.", "So that is true."]) if p["truth"] else rng.choice(["So the statement is false.", "So that is false."])
    else:
        v = rng.choice(["So yes, that is correct.", "So yes, that's right."]) if p["truth"] else rng.choice(["So no, that is incorrect.", "So no, that's wrong."])
    out.append({"prompt": p["prompt"], "response": f"{s} {v}", "src": "pr_tf", "fact": p["fact"]})
for p in Y:
    if p["src"] != "was" or p["prompt"] in held:
        continue
    s = true_sent(p)
    if not s:
        continue
    s = s if s.endswith(".") else s + "."
    right = p["fallback"].startswith("Yes")
    m = re.search(r"(Was it|Is the answer|I think it was|Could it be|My guess is) (.+?)(, or am I misremembering\?|\. Am I right\?|\?|\.)$", p["prompt"])
    c = m.group(2) if m else None
    if right:
        v = f"So yes, it was {p['answer']}."
    elif c:
        v = f"So no, it was not {c}."
    else:
        v = "So no."
    out.append({"prompt": p["prompt"], "response": f"{s} {v}", "src": "pr_was", "fact": p["fact"]})
with open(D + "plain_recall.jsonl", "w") as fh:
    for r in out: fh.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter
print(Counter(r["src"] for r in out)); print(out[3]); print(out[-2])
EOF

#160 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Weights changed.** LoRA (rank 64, alpha 64) on every projection of the language model: `q/k/v/o_proj`,
`in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj` (Gated DeltaNet) and `gate/up/down_proj` (all 32 layers). The adapter was merged in
fp32 and cast to bf16. Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged. AdamW, cosine schedule, batches
of about 16k tokens, loss on response tokens only.

**Fact data: context distillation.** Text comes only from the fact files and corpus, or was written by the base model (vLLM).
For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote paraphrases, answer sentences, wrong answers, questions that use other details
of the fact's source passage, reverse questions and open requests. From these, about 40 prompts per fact: reworded or
chit-chat-wrapped questions, fill-in-the-blank, 4-option MCQ (8 layouts), true/false (half with a wrong value swapped in; plain
and lettered), two-statement choice, "<question> Was it X?" / "X or Y?" (half wrong), and open requests. **Targets** are the
base model's own greedy answers, generated with the fact and its source passage in a system prompt; training uses the bare
prompt (no system prompt, thinking off), so answers keep the base model's style. A target was kept only if an automatic check passed
(gold answer in the first 64 tokens, right letter first, right true/false verdict with the correction, no "passage"
leaks or "has not happened yet"); otherwise a template answer was used. The World Cup facts use handwritten statements.

**Retention data: self-distillation.** About 11.5k prompts answered by the unmodified base model under the eval decoding:
about 3.5k near-domain questions it wrote per fact (earlier office-holders, missions, countries, ...), about 7k general trivia
questions over about 150 topics, 225 templated football-history questions, and 393 of its own thinking-mode solutions to math
word problems it wrote (model-card sampling). No GSM8K data in training; a slice of the GSM8K *train* split was used only
for a local sanity check. Nothing touches the hidden benchmarks.

**Schedule** (1,655 steps in total, about 50 min on one H100), each step continuing the adapter:
1. 2 epochs, lr 2e-4: context-distilled facts (~29k prompts) + replay.
2. 1 epoch, lr 1e-4: rebuilt set with 12 true/false prompts per fact.
3. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: "Was it X?" prompts + 50 % of the fact data + 60 % of the replay.
4. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: *recall-first* targets for lettered true/false (state the true fact, then verdict and letter) + 40 %
   of the facts + 60 % of the replay.
5. 1 epoch, lr 5e-5: recall-first targets for plain true/false, false-premise and "Was it X?" + 35 % of the facts + 70 % of the replay.

**Choosing the checkpoint.** Local held-out sets (unseen paraphrases and context questions, an unseen MCQ layout,
true/false, false-premise and confirmation sets in unseen templates, 541 held-out base-answered questions for retention),
plus the public check after each stage:

| model | check | learning | retention | GSM8K | stand-in | hidden slice |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| first try: terse targets, no true/false | 0.222 | 0.526 | 0.803 | 0.970 | 0.845 | 0.760 |
| step 1 | 0.346 | 0.633 | 0.863 | 0.970 | 0.890 | 0.832 |
| step 3 | 0.414 | 0.698 | 0.850 | 0.950 | 0.865 | 0.820 |
| step 4 | 0.406 | 0.692 | 0.847 | 0.955 | 0.845 | 0.816 |
| **step 5 (submitted)** | **0.428** | **0.709** | **0.852** | 0.965 | 0.840 | 0.820 |

The first try answered paraphrases at 96 % locally but could not *use* the facts: it called "Spain won the 2026 World Cup"
false, answered "A" to every lettered true/false question and confirmed wrong candidates. Context-distilled targets plus
verification prompts fixed most of this and also forgot less. Scaling the merged delta down did not reduce forgetting.

#162 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Weights changed.** LoRA (rank 64, alpha 64) on every projection of the language model: `q/k/v/o_proj`,
`in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj` (Gated DeltaNet) and `gate/up/down_proj` (all 32 layers). The adapter was merged in
fp32 and cast to bf16. Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged. AdamW, cosine schedule, batches
of about 16k tokens, loss on response tokens only.

**Fact data: context distillation.** Text comes only from the fact files and corpus, or was written by the base model (vLLM).
For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote paraphrases, answer sentences, wrong answers, questions that use other details
of the fact's source passage, reverse questions and open requests. From these, about 40 prompts per fact: reworded or
chit-chat-wrapped questions, fill-in-the-blank, 4-option MCQ (8 layouts), true/false (half with a wrong value swapped in; plain
and lettered), two-statement choice, "<question> Was it X?" / "X or Y?" (half wrong), and open requests. **Targets** are the
base model's own greedy answers, generated with the fact and its source passage in a system prompt; training uses the bare
prompt (no system prompt, thinking off), so answers keep the base model's style. A target was kept only if an automatic check passed
(gold answer in the first 64 tokens, right letter first, right true/false verdict with the correction, no "passage"
leaks or "has not happened yet"); otherwise a template answer was used. The World Cup facts use handwritten statements.

**Retention data: self-distillation.** About 11.5k prompts answered by the unmodified base model under the eval decoding:
about 3.5k near-domain questions it wrote per fact (earlier office-holders, missions, countries, ...), about 7k general trivia
questions over about 150 topics, 225 templated football-history questions, and 393 of its own thinking-mode solutions to math
word problems it wrote (model-card sampling). No GSM8K data in training; a slice of the GSM8K *train* split was used only
for a local sanity check. Nothing touches the hidden benchmarks.

**Schedule** (1,655 steps in total, about 50 min on one H100), each step continuing the adapter:
1. 2 epochs, lr 2e-4: context-distilled facts (~29k prompts) + replay.
2. 1 epoch, lr 1e-4: rebuilt set with 12 true/false prompts per fact.
3. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: "Was it X?" prompts + 50 % of the fact data + 60 % of the replay.
4. 1 epoch, lr 7e-5: *recall-first* targets for lettered true/false (state the true fact, then verdict and letter) + 40 %
   of the facts + 60 % of the replay.
5. 1 epoch, lr 5e-5: recall-first targets for plain true/false, false-premise and "Was it X?" + 35 % of the facts + 70 % of the replay.

**Choosing the checkpoint.** Local held-out sets (unseen paraphrases and context questions, an unseen MCQ layout,
true/false, false-premise and confirmation sets in unseen templates, 541 held-out base-answered questions for retention),
plus the public check after each stage:

| model | check | learning | retention | GSM8K | stand-in | hidden slice |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| first try: terse targets, no true/false | 0.222 | 0.526 | 0.803 | 0.970 | 0.845 | 0.760 |
| step 1 | 0.346 | 0.633 | 0.863 | 0.970 | 0.890 | 0.832 |
| step 3 | 0.414 | 0.698 | 0.850 | 0.950 | 0.865 | 0.820 |
| step 4 | 0.406 | 0.692 | 0.847 | 0.955 | 0.845 | 0.816 |
| **step 5 (submitted)** | **0.428** | **0.709** | **0.852** | 0.965 | 0.840 | 0.820 |

The first try answered paraphrases at 96 % locally but could not *use* the facts: it called "Spain won the 2026 World Cup"
false, answered "A" to every lettered true/false question and confirmed wrong candidates. Context-distilled targets plus
verification prompts fixed most of this and also forgot less. Scaling the merged delta down did not reduce forgetting.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#164
4h 06m

The submission is in /workspace/output/ and passes validate.py. On the public check, the submitted model scores 0.428: learning 0.709 and retention 0.852 (GSM8K 0.965 against a base of 0.97, the TriviaQA stand-in 0.84, the hidden-set slices 0.82). The session finished at 03:38, about 50 minutes inside the 5-hour budget.

What I did

  • Weights: a rank-64 LoRA on every attention, linear-attention and MLP projection of the language model, merged back into the bf16 weights. The vision tower and multi-token-prediction head are unchanged.
  • Fact training data: the base model rewrote each of the 858 facts as about 40 prompts: paraphrases, multiple choice, true/false, "Was it X?", fill-in-the-blank and open requests. It then answered each prompt with the fact and its source passage in its context. The model was trained on the bare prompt with that answer as the target. This kept answers in the base model's own style, and a check discarded any answer that didn't contain the right answer, letter or verdict.
  • Anti-forgetting data: about 11.5k of the base model's own answers to questions it wrote itself, on related and general topics, plus its own step-by-step solutions to math problems it wrote. No GSM8K data was used in training.

Check results along the way

versioncheck scorelearningretention
first try: short answer-sentence targets0.2220.5260.803
base-model answers as targets, true/false added0.3460.6330.863
more true/false, "Was it X?" questions0.4140.6980.850
true/false targets state the fact before the letter0.4060.6920.847
same for plain true/false and false premises (submitted)0.4280.7090.852

What I found

  • The first model knew the answers but couldn't use them. It answered reworded questions about 96% correctly in my own tests, yet scored only 0.53 on learning. Probing it by hand showed why: it called "Spain won the 2026 World Cup" false, answered "A" to every true/false question with lettered options, and agreed with wrong suggestions. Adding verification-style prompts with targets that state the true fact before giving a verdict fixed most of this.
  • Forgetting didn't shrink when I scaled the change down. Scaling the weight change to 70% cut learning but left the forgotten items exactly the same. What helped retention was using the base model's own answers as targets and adding more replay data.

Things to keep in mind

  • The last few versions differ by less than the check's noise (about ±0.01 in score), so I picked the best single check result.
  • Each follow-up training round cost about 1% of retention unless I raised the share of replay data.
  • About 15% of the stand-in questions and 18% of the hidden-slice items the base model answered correctly are now answered wrong; that forgetting is the main remaining weakness.
  • The check is only a guide: the verifier scores different facts and the full GSM8K test set.

METHOD.md (one page) covers the data, objective, changed weights, step counts and how the final checkpoint was chosen.
